You are auditing an experiment's code and records for bugs before anyone believes its results. Read-only; do not edit
files. Find shared helpers with grep and read them. Quote exact lines (file:line) for every claim.

For each checklist item give a verdict: **OK**, **BUG** (the fix and its effect on the numbers) or **DESIGN FLAW**
(what the result does and does not show). Then:
1. **Recompute every headline number** in the report independently from the raw records (runs.json / CSV / JSONL),
   with your own short script logic described in words, and say whether it matches.
2. Check that the verdict rules in code match the registered (frozen) predictions and falsifiers in the report.
3. Check for leakage: can any arm see the answer, the label, or test items during training or prompting?
4. Check fairness of comparisons: same budget, data, format, rank, steps, learning rate and selection rule for every
   arm that is compared; checkpoint selection only after every loss term is on.
5. Check that figures plot what their captions say.

Finish with: the BUGs by severity; which headline claims survive, in one line each; the minimal re-runs needed.
Be concise and exact.

## This experiment: PREDICT P2-v2 (directory: research/predict)
Report: the frozen "P2-v2 registration" section of PREDICT.md (commit 1504bf63) and its results (commit 34188284).
Code: pr_job.py, pr_data.py, pr_report.py and the shared harness (br_core.py, pb_lit.py, pb_pin.py). Results:
results/p2v2-savant-r3/.
Headline claims to check:
- In Savant's trained plain format, 16/16 draws are competent at delay 16; the bare format fails 16/16.
- The first-use margin falls from about 15.1 (τ=16) to 11.1 (8,192) and 10.75 (about 10k), but not monotonically
  (12.8 at 256 vs 13.6 at 2,048).
- Corruption test: the model never copies a corrupted history entry (0/16) and follows the table in every probe; the
  margin falls by 5–7 logits.
- Pinned twin: 3 full-history errors, all corrected by the pinned twin.
- Check:
  - that the trained format really is the harness format (imported builders, not a re-implementation);
  - that the first use of the held row is guaranteed at τ in the executed stream (not only the oracle);
  - that margins are read at the right token and against the right destination;
  - that the corruption overwrites exactly one earlier output for row r and that the reuse at the stated distance
    really uses row r;
  - that the pinned twin uses the same (state, event) as the erring step;
  - that the verdicts follow the frozen rules.

63:Swap test. A 4-row table, held row first. For u ∈ {1, 4, 16} and distances {64, 256, 1024, 4096, 8192}, u copies of `(q, star, s2)` sit just before the query, with filler in front so the query event is the target distance from the value token. The controlled condition feeds the old destination (teacher-forced history). The table token is then changed to s0. Follow-history means the argmax is the old destination. Follow-table means it is s0. The pin is one forward of the swapped 4-row table and is drawn at every distance; it is one measurement, not one per distance. The figure uses the controlled grid. A free-run swap after each stream is stored as `swap_free` and is not in the figure or in k3. The directional claim holds when the follow-history fraction, pooled over u, is higher at the longest distance present than at the shortest. The falsifier fires only past the horizon, and only when the follow-table fraction at that longest distance is ≥ 0.8 and the follow-history fraction is < 0.5. P2's verdict is the swap verdict.
65:Fits. Power: ln Δ = a + b ln n. Exponential: ln Δ = a + c · distance. Ordinary least squares, at least three positive-Δ points. A tie of SSE is untested. RoPE holds when the power SSE is smaller, b < 0, length scaling is off, and the RoPE falsifier has not fired. Length scaling makes the power-law verdict inapplicable (recorded as escape). The RoPE falsifier ratio is q(n_max) / q(n_min) with q(n) = Δ n / (1 + ln n)^{L−1}. It fires above 10. Steepening (late-half slope more negative than the early half) is required only when n_max ≥ e^{L−2}; otherwise it is "not yet" and is not a failure. ALiBi holds when the exponential SSE is smaller and r(d_long) / r(d_short) ≤ 1, with r(d) = Δ e^{min m · d} / d^{L−1} and min m the smallest positive per-head slope read off the ALiBi tensor. The per-head RoPE weight bound is reported as a fraction inside the interval, together with how many upper bounds exceed 1. It is not a separate verdict.
212:The horizon in §4.4 can sit past 32k. First-use concentration and error persistence are conditional on being past it; a clean run at these lengths does not confirm them, and a miss does not refute them. The depth factor (1 + ln n)^{L−1} hides the slope −1 until n is enormous, so a measured slope near −0.6 is the shape the note already describes, not a failed approach to −1. On BLOOM's 2048-token window the polynomial d^{L−1} can dominate e^{min m · d}, so the falsifier can stay silent while a power law still fits better than a pure exponential. The shape comparison is the one that can fail. e^{±2A}/n is often vacuous once 2A > ln n. A YaRN or LogN rope is an escape from H1, not a counterexample. The controlled swap feeds the old destination, which is the history in the proposition; the free-run swap confounds u with distance and is not the figure. The pin can fail to read the table even when Savant certified the names. sdpa is not bitwise across two calls; the identity gate is the recorded tolerance, not a silent loosening.
220:- bloom-1b1: the polynomial d^(L-1) grows faster than e^(min m · d) on this grid, so a slow decay need not trip the §4.3 falsifier. The shape fit is the comparison that can.

246:## P2-v2 registration
248:Registered before any P2-v2 spend. P1 and P3 are not rerun. The job is `p2v2-savant-r3` in `pr_v2.py`: Savant r3 only, seed 20261028, adapter `/arvol/arith-s1r2/adapter_step500`. 260:The corruption test replaces the swap test. On a teacher-forced trajectory one earlier destination of the held row is overwritten with a different state token. The next use is g ≥ 2 events later, so the overwritten token is not the state token of that reuse. The token distance is `g * stride − 1`. Both formats have stride 2, so the aims 64, 512 and 4096 are the distances 65, 513 and 4097. The largest g that fits is included when its distance is not already one of those. The uncorrupted twin is scored at the same reuse. `follow_h` is the overwritten token, `follow_t` is the table's destination, and neither is the rest.
262:The fall is the median, across the passing draws, of the teacher-forced first-use margin at each exposed τ. It falls when every longer τ has a strictly smaller median. The flat-or-rising falsifier is that comparison in the trained format. Fewer than two τ is untested. Free-run margins are reported and are not that falsifier.
264:At the shortest recorded corruption distance, the copy rate is the fraction of draws with `follow_h` 1 and the control error rate is the fraction whose uncorrupted twin is wrong. The copy claim holds when the copy rate is higher. The falsifier fires when `follow_h` is 0 at every recorded distance. The pin claim holds when the pin is correct on every recorded full-history error. Its falsifier fires when the pin is wrong on every one of them. No errors leaves the pin claim untested.
266:Work order is the gate, then for each passing draw the teacher-forced ladder, the corruption distances, and the free-runs, trained format before bare. The process stops 90 seconds before `max_seconds` and keeps the cells already written. Step logs are separate json files. The summary is `runs.json`. These results stay unaudited.

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""
     2	P2-v2. Savant r3 only. Two formats, a delayed first use, a pinned twin on errors, and a corruption test.
     3	
     4	The trained format is br_core.layout of a cycle program from pb_families. The bare format is the
     5	16-row table in pr_data. Neither builder is reimplemented here.
     6	
     7	    python research/predict/pr_v2.py --selftest
     8	    python research/predict/pr_v2.py --out /vol/p2v2-savant-r3 --max-seconds 28723
     9	"""
    10	
    11	from __future__ import annotations
    12	
    13	import json
    14	import os
    15	import pathlib
    16	import random
    17	import sys
    18	import time
    19	
    20	HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    21	PB = HERE.parent / "promptbox"
    22	for _p in (str(HERE), str(PB)):
    23	    if _p not in sys.path:
    24	        sys.path.insert(0, _p)
    25	
    26	import br_core  # noqa: E402
    27	import pb_families  # noqa: E402
    28	import pb_lit  # noqa: E402
    29	import pr_data  # noqa: E402
    30	import pr_job  # noqa: E402
    31	from pb_data import chat_prompt  # noqa: E402
    32	from pb_lang import parse_program, step_register  # noqa: E402
    33	
    34	SEED = pr_data.SEED
    35	MODEL = "savant-r3"
    36	N_DRAWS = 4
    37	MAX_K = 16
    38	GATE_LEN = 16
    39	FIXED_TAUS = (16, 256, 2048, 8192)
    40	CORRUPT_TARGETS = (64, 512, 4096)
    41	REUSES = 4
    42	BARE_STRIDE = 2
    43	
    44	
    45	def can_reach(transit, states, events, end, steps, forbidden):
    46	    """can[k] holds the states that can reach `end` in exactly k events without taking `forbidden`."""
    47	    can = [set() for _ in range(steps + 1)]
    48	    can[0].add(end)
    49	    for left in range(steps):
    50	        for s in states:
    51	            for e in events:
    52	                if (s, e) == forbidden:
    53	                    continue
    54	                if transit(s, e) in can[left]:
    55	                    can[left + 1].add(s)
    56	    return can
    57	
    58	
    59	def path(transit, states, events, start, steps, end, forbidden):
    60	    """Exactly `steps` events from start to end, never taking the forbidden pair. None if there is no such walk."""
    61	    if steps < 0:
    62	        return None
    63	    can = can_reach(transit, states, events, end, steps, forbidden)
    64	    if start not in can[steps]:
    65	        return None
    66	    state = start
    67	    out = []
    68	    for left in range(steps, 0, -1):
    69	        nxt_e = None
    70	        for e in events:
    71	            if (state, e) == forbidden:
    72	                continue
    73	            nxt = transit(state, e)
    74	            if nxt in can[left - 1]:
    75	                nxt_e = e
    76	                break
    77	        if nxt_e is None:
    78	            return None
    79	        out.append(nxt_e)
    80	        state = transit(state, nxt_e)
    81	    return out if state == end else None
    82	
    83	
    84	def trace_pairs(transit, start, events):
    85	    state = start
    86	    out = []
    87	    for event in events:
    88	        dest = transit(state, event)
    89	        out.append((state, event, dest))
    90	        state = dest
    91	    return out
    92	
    93	
    94	def held_indexes(pairs, held):
    95	    return [i for i, (s, e, _d) in enumerate(pairs) if (s, e) == held]
    96	
    97	
    98	def first_use_events(transit, states, events, start, tau, held):
    99	    """Events whose first use of `held` is at index tau, plus REUSES later uses of the same row."""
   100	    s_star, e_star = held
   101	    prefix = [] if tau == 0 and start == s_star else path(transit, states, events, start, tau, s_star, held)
   102	    if prefix is None:
   103	        return None
   104	    out = list(prefix) + [e_star]
   105	    state = transit(s_star, e_star)
   106	    for _ in range(REUSES):
   107	        gap = min_return(transit, states, events, state, s_star, held)
   108	        if gap is None:
   109	            return None
   110	        mid, state = gap
   111	        out.extend(mid)
   112	        out.append(e_star)
   113	        state = transit(s_star, e_star)
   114	    pairs = trace_pairs(transit, start, out)
   115	    uses = held_indexes(pairs, held)
   116	    if not uses or uses[0] != tau or len(uses) != 1 + REUSES:
   117	        return None
   118	    return out
   119	
   120	
   121	def min_return(transit, states, events, start, end, forbidden):
   122	    """Shortest non-empty return: events that avoid `forbidden`, land on `end`, then the caller appends the held event.
   123	    A zero-step return is allowed when start is already `end` (the next event can be the held event)."""
   124	    if start == end:
   125	        return [], start
   126	    for steps in range(1, len(states) * len(events) + 2):
   127	        mid = path(transit, states, events, start, steps, end, forbidden)
   128	        if mid is not None:
   129	            return mid, end
   130	    return None
   131	
   132	
   133	def gap_for(target, stride):
   134	    """Smallest positive event gap whose token distance, g * stride - 1, is at least `target`."""
   135	    g = 1
   136	    while g * stride - 1 < target:
   137	        g += 1
   138	    return g
   139	
   140	
   141	def token_distance(g, stride):
   142	    return g * stride - 1
   143	
   144	
   145	def corruption_events(transit, states, events, start, held, g):
   146	    """Two uses of `held`, the second g events after the first, and no use between them. g >= 2."""
   147	    if g < 2:
   148	        return None
   149	    s_star, e_star = held
   150	    first = None
   151	    prefix = None
   152	    for t in range(0, len(states) * len(events) + 2):
   153	        if t == 0 and start == s_star:
   154	            prefix = []
   155	        else:
   156	            prefix = path(transit, states, events, start, t, s_star, held)
   157	        if prefix is not None:
   158	            first = t
   159	            break
   160	    if prefix is None:
   161	        return None
   162	    post = transit(s_star, e_star)
   163	    mid = [] if g == 1 else path(transit, states, events, post, g - 1, s_star, held)
   164	    if mid is None:
   165	        return None
   166	    out = list(prefix) + [e_star] + list(mid) + [e_star]
   167	    pairs = trace_pairs(transit, start, out)
   168	    uses = held_indexes(pairs, held)
   169	    if uses != [first, first + g]:
   170	        return None
   171	    return out, first
   172	
   173	
   174	def euler_events(transit, states, events, start):
   175	    """Each (state, event) once, if the graph has a directed Eulerian circuit from start."""
   176	    unused = {s: [(e, transit(s, e)) for e in events] for s in states}
   177	    stack = [(start, None)]
   178	    circ = []
   179	    while stack:
   180	        state, via = stack[-1]
   181	        if unused[state]:
   182	            event, nxt = unused[state].pop()
   183	            stack.append((nxt, event))
   184	        else:
   185	            stack.pop()
   186	            if via is not None:
   187	                circ.append(via)
   188	    circ.reverse()
   189	    pairs = trace_pairs(transit, start, circ)
   190	    need = {(s, e) for s in states for e in events}
   191	    if len(circ) != len(need) or {(s, e) for s, e, _d in pairs} != need:
   192	        return None
   193	    return circ
   194	
   195	
   196	def covering_or_none(transit, states, events, start, length):
   197	    """A walk of exactly `length` that uses every row at least once, or None."""
   198	    circ = euler_events(transit, states, events, start)
   199	    if circ is None or len(circ) > length:
   200	        return None
   201	    state = start
   202	    for event in circ:
   203	        state = transit(state, event)
   204	    out = list(circ)
   205	    while len(out) < length:
   206	        event = events[0]
   207	        out.append(event)
   208	        state = transit(state, event)
   209	    pairs = trace_pairs(transit, start, out)
   210	    need = {(s, e) for s in states for e in events}
   211	    if {(s, e) for s, e, _d in pairs} != need or len(out) != length:
   212	        return None
   213	    return out
   214	
   215	
   216	def bare_tau_max(max_pos, pi_len):
   217	    """Largest even tau >= 16 whose events_for_delay tape, including four reuses, fits in the window."""
   218	    room = (int(max_pos) - int(pi_len)) // BARE_STRIDE - 1 - 2 * pr_data.REUSES
   219	    room -= room % 2
   220	    if room < 16:
   221	        return None
   222	    n_events = room + 1 + 2 * pr_data.REUSES
   223	    if pi_len + BARE_STRIDE * n_events > max_pos:
   224	        return None
   225	    return room
   226	
   227	
   228	def ladder_for(tau_max):
   229	    if tau_max is None:
   230	        return []
   231	    out = [t for t in FIXED_TAUS if t <= tau_max]
   232	    if tau_max not in out:
   233	        out.append(tau_max)
   234	    return out
   235	
   236	
   237	def choose_held(transit, states, events, start):
   238	    cands = [(s, e) for s in states for e in events]
   239	    cands.sort(key=lambda se: (transit(se[0], se[1]) != se[0], se[0] == start))
   240	    for held in cands:
   241	        if first_use_events(transit, states, events, start, 16, held) is None:
   242	            continue
   243	        if first_use_events(transit, states, events, start, 256, held) is None:
   244	            continue
   245	        return held
   246	    return None
   247	
   248	
   249	def _atomic(path, text):
   250	    path = pathlib.Path(path)
   251	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   252	    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + f".{os.getpid()}.tmp")
   253	    tmp.write_text(text)
   254	    os.replace(tmp, path)
   255	
   256	
   257	def save_state(out, state):
   258	    blob = json.dumps(state, allow_nan=False, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True)
   259	    if len(blob.encode()) > 1_800_000:
   260	        raise RuntimeError(f"runs.json is {len(blob)} bytes")
   261	    _atomic(pathlib.Path(out) / "runs.json", blob)
   262	
   263	
   264	def _local_tokenizer():
   265	    import glob
   266	    paths = glob.glob(str(pathlib.Path.home() / ".cache/huggingface/hub/models--Qwen--Qwen3-4B/snapshots/*/tokenizer.json"))
   267	    if not paths:
   268	        return None
   269	    try:
   270	        from tokenizers import Tokenizer
   271	    except ImportError:
   272	        return None
   273	    return Tokenizer.from_file(paths[0])
   274	
   275	
   276	class OffTok:
   277	    """tokenizers.Tokenizer, the object br_core.layout and pb_lit.walk both read (.ids and .offsets)."""
   278	
   279	    def __init__(self, raw):
   280	        self.raw = raw
   281	
   282	    def get_vocab(self):
   283	        return self.raw.get_vocab()
   284	
   285	    def encode(self, text, add_special_tokens=False):
   286	        return self.raw.encode(text, add_special_tokens=add_special_tokens)
   287	
   288	
   289	def _cycle_machine(inst):
   290	    prog = parse_program(inst.program_text)
   291	    if prog.other or len(prog.registers) != 1:
   292	        return None
   293	    reg = prog.registers[0]
   294	    events = list(prog.event_classes())
   295	    states = list(reg.values)
   296	    if len(states) * len(events) > GATE_LEN:
   297	        return None
   298	    start = prog.start[reg.name]
   299	
   300	    def transit(value, event, _prog=prog, _reg=reg):
   301	        return step_register(_prog, _reg, value, event)
   302	
   303	    return prog, reg, states, events, start, transit
   304	
   305	
   306	def draw_trained(tok, k, seed):
   307	    """One cycle program the literal builder accepts, plus a closure that lays a tape out in the trained format."""
   308	    rng = random.Random(f"{seed}|p2v2|{k}")
   309	    reasons = []
   310	    for _try in range(60):
   311	        inst = pb_families.cycle(rng, 2, split="train", fmt="lines")
   312	        machine = _cycle_machine(inst)
   313	        if machine is None:
   314	            reasons.append("rows or shape")
   315	            continue
   316	        _prog, reg, states, events, start, transit = machine
   317	        short = pb_families.render_stream(inst.items, inst.fmt)
   318	        if inst.question.count(short) != 1:
   319	            reasons.append("stream not unique")
   320	            continue
   321	        held = choose_held(transit, states, events, start)
   322	        if held is None:
   323	            reasons.append("no delayable row")
   324	            continue
   325	
   326	        def make(items, _inst=inst, _short=short, _reg=reg):
   327	            question = _inst.question.replace(_short, pb_families.render_stream(items, _inst.fmt), 1)
   328	            ex = {"prompt": chat_prompt(question), "question": question, "prog_text": _inst.program_text,
   329	                  "items": list(items), "answer": _inst.answer}
   330	            lit, why = pb_lit.literal(ex, tok)
   331	            if lit is None:
   332	                return None, why
   333	            if len(lit["registers"]) != 1:
   334	                return None, "not one register"
   335	            lay = br_core.layout(tok, ex, lit)
   336	            if len(lay["tape_pos"]) != lay["T"] or lay["R"] != 1 or lay["stride"] != 2:
   337	                return None, "layout"
   338	            return {"ex": ex, "lit": lit, "lay": lay, "reg": _reg.name}, None
   339	
   340	        probe, why = make(inst.items)
   341	        if probe is None:
   342	            reasons.append(why or "probe")
   343	            continue
   344	        names = probe["lit"]["registers"][0]["name_of"]
   345	        held_names = (names[held[0]], held[1])
   346	        return {
   347	            "k": k, "held": held, "held_names": held_names, "states": states, "events": events,
   348	            "start": start, "transit": transit, "make": make, "task": probe["lit"]["task"],
   349	            "fmt": inst.fmt, "n_rows": len(states) * len(events),
   350	            "program": probe["lit"]["registers"][0]["name"],
   351	        }
   352	    return {"k": k, "error": "no trainable cycle", "reasons": reasons[-8:]}
   353	
   354	
   355	def _queries_trained(lay, start_id):
   356	    rs, stride, ids = lay["run_start"], lay["stride"], lay["ids"]
   357	    out = []
   358	    for k in range(lay["T"]):
   359	        epos = rs + k * stride
   360	        if k == 0:
   361	            state_pos, state_id = -1, start_id
   362	        else:
   363	            state_pos = rs + (k - 1) * stride + 1
   364	            state_id = ids[state_pos]
   365	        out.append({"step": k, "pos": epos, "state_pos": state_pos, "state": state_id,
   366	                    "event": ids[epos], "true": ids[epos + 1]})
   367	    return out
   368	
   369	
   370	def _queries_bare(pi_len, events, id_of, transit, start):
   371	    pos = pi_len
   372	    state = start
   373	    out = []
   374	    ids_state = []
   375	    for k, event in enumerate(events):
   376	        dest = transit(state, event)
   377	        out.append({"step": k, "pos": pos + 1, "state_pos": pos, "state": id_of[state],
   378	                    "event": id_of[event], "true": id_of[dest], "state_name": state, "event_name": event})
   379	        ids_state.append(state)
   380	        pos += BARE_STRIDE
   381	        state = dest
   382	    return out
   383	
   384	
   385	def bare_ids(pi, events, id_of, transit, start):
   386	    ids = list(pi)
   387	    state = start
   388	    for event in events:
   389	        ids.extend((id_of[state], id_of[event]))
   390	        state = transit(state, event)
   391	    return ids
   392	
   393	
   394	def fit_even_tau(measure, lo, hi):
   395	    """Largest even tau in [lo, hi] for which measure(tau) is true. measure must be monotone in tau."""
   396	    if not measure(lo):
   397	        return None
   398	    best = lo
   399	    while lo <= hi:
   400	        mid = ((lo + hi) // 2)
   401	        mid -= mid % 2
   402	        if mid < lo:
   403	            break
   404	        if measure(mid):
   405	            best = mid
   406	            lo = mid + 2
   407	        else:
   408	            hi = mid - 2
   409	    return best
   410	
   411	
   412	def _selftest():
   413	    def transit(state, event):
   414	        order = ["red", "green", "yellow"]
   415	        i = order.index(state)
   416	        if event == "next":
   417	            return order[(i + 1) % 3]
   418	        if event == "back":
   419	            return order[(i - 1) % 3]
   420	        if event == "stay":
   421	            return state
   422	        raise AssertionError(event)
   423	
   424	    states, events, start = ["red", "green", "yellow"], ["next", "back", "stay"], "red"
   425	    held = choose_held(transit, states, events, start)
   426	    assert held is not None
   427	    for tau in (16, 256, 2048):
   428	        ev = first_use_events(transit, states, events, start, tau, held)
   429	        assert ev is not None
   430	        uses = held_indexes(trace_pairs(transit, start, ev), held)
   431	        assert uses[0] == tau and len(uses) == 1 + REUSES
   432	    assert gap_for(64, 2) == 33 and token_distance(33, 2) == 65
   433	    assert gap_for(512, 2) == 257 and token_distance(257, 2) == 513
   434	    assert gap_for(4096, 2) == 2049 and token_distance(2049, 2) == 4097
   435	    built = corruption_events(transit, states, events, start, held, 33)
   436	    assert built is not None
   437	    ev, first = built
   438	    uses = held_indexes(trace_pairs(transit, start, ev), held)
   439	    assert uses == [first, first + 33]
   440	    cover = covering_or_none(transit, states, events, start, 16)
   441	    assert cover is not None and len(cover) == 16
   442	    seen = {(s, e) for s, e, _d in trace_pairs(transit, start, cover)}
   443	    assert seen == {(s, e) for s in states for e in events}
   444	
   445	    bare_t = lambda s, e: pr_data.DELTA[(s, e)]
   446	    assert covering_or_none(bare_t, pr_data.STATES, pr_data.EVENTS, "s0", 16) is None
   447	    bare_held = ("q", "star")
   448	    for g in (33, 257):
   449	        built = corruption_events(bare_t, pr_data.STATES, pr_data.EVENTS, "s0", bare_held, g)
   450	        assert built is not None, g
   451	        ev, first = built
   452	        assert held_indexes(trace_pairs(bare_t, "s0", ev), bare_held) == [first, first + g]
   453	    assert bare_tau_max(40960, 64) == 20438
   454	    assert ladder_for(20438) == [16, 256, 2048, 8192, 20438]
   455	    assert ladder_for(2048) == [16, 256, 2048]
   456	    assert fit_even_tau(lambda t: t <= 100, 16, 200) == 100
   457	    assert fit_even_tau(lambda t: t <= 99, 16, 200) == 98
   458	    assert fit_even_tau(lambda t: False, 16, 40) is None
   459	
   460	    tok = _local_tokenizer()
   461	    if tok is None:
   462	        print(json.dumps({"selftest": "ok", "layout": "skipped"}))
   463	        return
   464	    wrapped = OffTok(tok)
   465	    drawn = draw_trained(wrapped, 0, SEED)
   466	    assert "error" not in drawn, drawn.get("reasons")
   467	    ev = first_use_events(drawn["transit"], drawn["states"], drawn["events"], drawn["start"], 16, drawn["held"])
   468	    made, why = drawn["make"](ev)
   469	    assert made is not None, why
   470	    lay = made["lay"]
   471	    assert lay["n"] <= 40960
   472	    start_id = pb_lit.name_id(wrapped, made["lit"]["registers"][0]["start"])
   473	    queries = _queries_trained(lay, start_id)
   474	    uses = [q["step"] for q in queries
   475	            if q["state"] == pb_lit.name_id(wrapped, drawn["held_names"][0])
   476	            and q["event"] == pb_lit.name_id(wrapped, drawn["held_names"][1])]
   477	    assert uses[0] == 16, uses[:6]
   478	    # the corrupted token of use 0 is not the state token of use g
   479	    g = 33
   480	    built = corruption_events(drawn["transit"], drawn["states"], drawn["events"], drawn["start"], drawn["held"], g)
   481	    assert built is not None
   482	    cev, first = built
   483	    cmade, why = drawn["make"](cev)
   484	    assert cmade is not None, why
   485	    clay = cmade["lay"]
   486	    cqueries = _queries_trained(clay, start_id)
   487	    out_pos = clay["run_start"] + first * clay["stride"] + 1
   488	    reuse = cqueries[first + g]
   489	    assert reuse["state_pos"] != out_pos
   490	    assert reuse["pos"] - out_pos == token_distance(g, clay["stride"])
   491	    print(json.dumps({"selftest": "ok", "layout": "ok", "held": list(drawn["held_names"]),
   492	                      "rows": drawn["n_rows"], "tau16_tokens": lay["n"]}))
   493	
   494	
   495	def logits_at(model, ids, positions, chunk=64):
   496	    import torch
   497	    x = torch.tensor([list(ids)], device=model.device)
   498	    with torch.no_grad():
   499	        hidden = model.model(input_ids=x, use_cache=False).last_hidden_state[0]
   500	    cols = []
   501	    for i in range(0, len(positions), chunk):
   502	        sl = positions[i:i + chunk]
   503	        cols.append(model.lm_head(hidden[sl]).float())
   504	    out = torch.cat(cols, 0) if cols else hidden[:0]
   505	    del hidden, cols, x
   506	    return out
   507	
   508	
   509	class Pin:
   510	    def __init__(self, model, pi):
   511	        self.model = model
   512	        self.pi = list(pi)
   513	        self.past = None
   514	        self.cache = {}
   515	
   516	    def query(self, state, event, true):
   517	        key = (int(state), int(event))
   518	        if key in self.cache:
   519	            return self.cache[key]
   520	        import pb_pin
   521	        if self.past is None:
   522	            self.past = pb_pin.prefill(self.model, self.pi)
   523	        lg = pb_pin.tick(self.model, self.past, len(self.pi), int(state), int(event))
   524	        pred, margin, ok = pr_job.decision(lg.float(), int(true))
   525	        del lg
   526	        rec = (int(pred), round(float(margin), 4), int(ok))
   527	        self.cache[key] = rec
   528	        return rec
   529	
   530	
   531	def _pack(step, pos, state, event, pred, true, margin, pin):
   532	    return [int(step), int(pos), int(state), int(event), int(pred), None if true is None else int(true),
   533	            None if margin is None else round(float(margin), 4), None if pin is None else int(pin)]
   534	
   535	
   536	def _write_steps(out, name, rows):
   537	    blob = json.dumps(rows, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)
   538	    if len(blob.encode()) > 1_700_000:
   539	        raise RuntimeError(f"{name} is {len(blob)} bytes")
   540	    _atomic(pathlib.Path(out) / "steps" / name, blob)
   541	
   542	
   543	def _score_tf(model, ids, queries, pin, tau, out, name):
   544	    import torch
   545	    positions = [q["pos"] for q in queries]
   546	    try:
   547	        lgs = logits_at(model, ids, positions)
   548	    except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   549	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   550	        return None, "oom"
   551	    rows = []
   552	    n_err = pin_err = pin_n = 0
   553	    first = None
   554	    diverged = False
   555	    for i, q in enumerate(queries):
   556	        pred, margin, ok = pr_job.decision(lgs[i], q["true"])
   557	        pin_ok = None
   558	        if not ok:
   559	            n_err += 1
   560	            if q["step"] < tau:
   561	                diverged = True
   562	            _pred, _m, pok = pin.query(q["state"], q["event"], q["true"])
   563	            pin_ok = pok
   564	            pin_n += 1
   565	            pin_err += int(not pok)
   566	        if q.get("held") and first is None:
   567	            first = {"step": q["step"], "margin": round(float(margin), 4), "ok": int(ok), "pred": int(pred)}
   568	        rows.append(_pack(q["step"], q["pos"], q["state"], q["event"], pred, q["true"], margin, pin_ok))
   569	    del lgs
   570	    _write_steps(out, name, rows)
   571	    # Teacher forcing writes the true state, so the held row is fed even if an earlier step was wrong.
   572	    return {"first": first, "n_err": n_err, "n": len(rows), "pin_n": pin_n, "pin_err": pin_err,
   573	            "diverged_before_exposure": 0 if first is not None else 1,
   574	            "exposed": int(first is not None)}, None
   575	
   576	
   577	def _one(model, past, pos, tok_id):
   578	    import torch
   579	    pos_ids = torch.tensor([[pos]], device=model.device)
   580	    with torch.no_grad():
   581	        out_m = model(input_ids=torch.tensor([[int(tok_id)]], device=model.device), position_ids=pos_ids,
   582	                      cache_position=pos_ids[0], past_key_values=past, use_cache=True, logits_to_keep=1)
   583	    return out_m.past_key_values, out_m.logits[0, -1].float()
   584	
   585	
   586	def _free_run(model, past, pos0, queries, pin, tau, held_pair, dest_of, start_state, kind, out, name, t0, limit):
   587	    """Free-running decode. `trained` feeds the event and then the predicted state. `bare` feeds the state and
   588	    then the event; the predicted destination is the next state. An error before τ stops the arm: the held row
   589	    was not exposed."""
   590	    import torch
   591	    rows = []
   592	    n_err = pin_err = pin_n = 0
   593	    first = None
   594	    diverged = False
   595	    state_id = int(start_state)
   596	    pos = int(pos0)
   597	    try:
   598	        for q in queries:
   599	            if time.monotonic() - t0 > limit - 90:
   600	                _write_steps(out, name, rows)
   601	                return {"partial": 1, "n": len(rows), "diverged_before_exposure": 1, "exposed": 0}, "budget"
   602	            event = int(q["event"])
   603	            if kind == "bare":
   604	                past, _lg = _one(model, past, pos, state_id)
   605	                del _lg
   606	                pos += 1
   607	            past, lg = _one(model, past, pos, event)
   608	            true = dest_of.get((state_id, event))
   609	            held = state_id == int(held_pair[0]) and event == int(held_pair[1])
   610	            if true is None:
   611	                pred = int(torch.argmax(lg))
   612	                margin, ok, pin_ok = None, True, None
   613	                if q["step"] < tau:
   614	                    diverged = True
   615	            else:
   616	                pred, margin, ok = pr_job.decision(lg, true)
   617	                pin_ok = None
   618	                if not ok:
   619	                    n_err += 1
   620	                    _pred, _m, pok = pin.query(state_id, event, true)
   621	                    pin_ok = pok
   622	                    pin_n += 1
   623	                    pin_err += int(not pok)
   624	                    if q["step"] < tau:
   625	                        diverged = True
   626	            del lg
   627	            if held and first is None:
   628	                first = {"step": q["step"], "margin": None if margin is None else round(float(margin), 4),
   629	                         "ok": int(bool(ok) and true is not None and pred == true), "pred": int(pred)}
   630	            rows.append(_pack(q["step"], pos, state_id, event, pred, true, margin, pin_ok))
   631	            if kind == "trained":
   632	                pos += 1
   633	                past, _lg = _one(model, past, pos, pred)
   634	                del _lg
   635	                pos += 1
   636	            else:
   637	                pos += 1
   638	            state_id = int(pred)
   639	            if diverged and q["step"] < tau:
   640	                break
   641	            if q["step"] and q["step"] % 256 == 0:
   642	                print(json.dumps({"free": name, "step": q["step"], "pos": pos}), flush=True)
   643	    except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   644	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   645	        _write_steps(out, name, rows)
   646	        return {"partial": 1, "n": len(rows), "exposed": 0, "diverged_before_exposure": 1}, "oom"
   647	    _write_steps(out, name, rows)
   648	    exposed = first is not None and not (diverged and (first["step"] < tau))
   649	    # An error at the held step is exposure. An error before it is not.
   650	    if diverged and (first is None or first["step"] < tau):
   651	        exposed = False
   652	        first = None
   653	    return {"first": first, "n_err": n_err, "n": len(rows), "pin_n": pin_n, "pin_err": pin_err,
   654	            "diverged_before_exposure": int(not exposed), "exposed": int(exposed)}, None
   655	
   656	
   657	def _mark_held(queries, state_id, event_id, tau):
   658	    for q in queries:
   659	        q["held"] = int(q["state"] == state_id and q["event"] == event_id and q["step"] >= 0)
   660	    # the first held step is the exposure; later ones are reuses and stay marked
   661	    return queries
   662	
   663	
   664	def _overdue(t0, limit):
   665	    return time.monotonic() - t0 > limit - 90
   666	
   667	
   668	def run(args):
   669	    import torch
   670	    t0 = time.monotonic()
   671	    out = pathlib.Path(args.out)
   672	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   673	    prior = out / "runs.json"
   674	    state = json.loads(prior.read_text()) if prior.is_file() else {}
   675	    state.update({"schema": 2, "mode": "p2v2", "model": MODEL, "seed": int(args.seed), "unaudited": True,
   676	                  "complete": False})
   677	    print(json.dumps({"load": MODEL}), flush=True)
   678	    model, tok, snapshot, merged = pr_job.load_model(MODEL, args.adapter)
   679	    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
   680	    raw = Tokenizer.from_file(str(pathlib.Path(snapshot) / "tokenizer.json"))
   681	    otok = OffTok(raw)
   682	    state["snapshot"] = snapshot
   683	    state["adapter"] = merged
   684	    state["config"] = pr_job.config_record(model)
   685	    state["host"] = pr_job.host_record()
   686	    max_pos = int(state["config"]["max_position_embeddings"])
   687	    state["max_position_embeddings"] = max_pos
   688	    print(json.dumps({"config": {"max_position_embeddings": max_pos,
   689	                                 "sliding_window": state["config"].get("sliding_window")}}), flush=True)
   690	    pool = pr_job.load_pool(tok)
   691	    state.setdefault("cells", {})
   692	    state.setdefault("corruption", [])
   693	    state.setdefault("draws", [])
   694	    limit = int(args.max_seconds)
   695	
   696	    def persist():
   697	        save_state(out, state)
   698	
   699	    have = {d["k"]: d for d in state["draws"]}
   700	    passing = {"trained": [], "bare": []}
   701	    k = 0
   702	    while k < MAX_K and (len(passing["trained"]) < N_DRAWS or len(passing["bare"]) < N_DRAWS):
   703	        if _overdue(t0, limit):
   704	            state["budget_stop"] = "during gates"
   705	            break
   706	        rec = have.get(k)
   707	        if rec is None:
   708	            rec = {"k": k}
   709	            bare = pr_job._table_from_pool(pool, tok, k, int(args.seed))
   710	            rows = pr_data.rows_state_major()
   711	            pi, spans = pr_data.encode_rows(rows, bare["id_of"], bare["nl"])
   712	            rec["bare"] = {"names": bare["names"], "pi_len": len(pi)}
   713	            trained = draw_trained(otok, k, int(args.seed))
   714	            if "error" in trained:
   715	                rec["trained"] = {"error": trained["error"], "reasons": trained.get("reasons")}
   716	            else:
   717	                rec["trained"] = {"held": list(trained["held_names"]), "n_rows": trained["n_rows"],
   718	                                  "task": trained["task"], "fmt": trained["fmt"], "start": trained["start"]}
   719	            # gates
   720	            rec["gate"] = {}
   721	            for fmt in ("trained", "bare"):
   722	                rec["gate"][fmt] = _gate(model, fmt, rec, trained if "error" not in trained else None,
   723	                                         bare, rows, pi, otok, max_pos)
   724	            state["draws"].append(rec)
   725	            have[k] = rec
   726	            persist()
   727	            print(json.dumps({"gate": k, "trained": rec["gate"]["trained"].get("pass"),
   728	                              "bare": rec["gate"]["bare"].get("pass")}), flush=True)
   729	        for fmt in ("trained", "bare"):
   730	            if rec["gate"][fmt].get("pass") and k not in passing[fmt] and len(passing[fmt]) < N_DRAWS:
   731	                passing[fmt].append(k)
   732	        k += 1
   733	    state["passing"] = passing
   734	    state["dropped"] = [fmt for fmt in ("trained", "bare") if not passing[fmt]]
   735	    persist()
   736	
   737	    for fmt in ("trained", "bare"):
   738	        if fmt in state["dropped"]:
   739	            continue
   740	        for k in passing[fmt]:
   741	            if _overdue(t0, limit):
   742	                state["budget_stop"] = f"before {fmt} d{k}"
   743	                persist()
   744	                break
   745	            _run_draw(model, state, out, fmt, k, otok, tok, pool, max_pos, t0, limit, persist)
   746	        if state.get("budget_stop"):
   747	            break
   748	    state["complete"] = not bool(state.get("budget_stop"))
   749	    state["seconds"] = round(time.monotonic() - t0, 3)
   750	    persist()
   751	    print(json.dumps({"complete": state["complete"], "budget": state.get("budget_stop"),
   752	                      "seconds": state["seconds"]}), flush=True)
   753	
   754	
   755	def _gate(model, fmt, rec, trained, bare, rows, pi, otok, max_pos):
   756	    import torch
   757	    if fmt == "trained":
   758	        if trained is None:
   759	            return {"pass": 0, "why": "no program"}
   760	        cover = covering_or_none(trained["transit"], trained["states"], trained["events"], trained["start"], GATE_LEN)
   761	        if cover is None:
   762	            ev = first_use_events(trained["transit"], trained["states"], trained["events"],
   763	                                  trained["start"], GATE_LEN, trained["held"])
   764	            kind = "first-use"
   765	        else:
   766	            ev = cover
   767	            kind = "covering"
   768	        if ev is None:
   769	            return {"pass": 0, "why": "no gate tape"}
   770	        made, why = trained["make"](ev)
   771	        if made is None:
   772	            return {"pass": 0, "why": why}
   773	        lay = made["lay"]
   774	        if lay["n"] > max_pos:
   775	            return {"pass": 0, "why": "gate exceeds context"}
   776	        reg = made["lit"]["registers"][0]
   777	        id_of = {n: pb_lit.name_id(otok, n) for n in list(reg["name_of"].values()) + made["lit"]["events"]}
   778	        pin_rows = [(s, e, t) for s, e, t in reg["rows"]]
   779	        program = lay["ids"][lay["prog_start"]:lay["prog_start"] + lay["prog_len"]]
   780	        try:
   781	            pins = pr_job.pin_rows(model, program, pin_rows, id_of)
   782	        except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   783	            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   784	            return {"pass": 0, "why": "oom"}
   785	        start_id = pb_lit.name_id(otok, reg["start"])
   786	        queries = _queries_trained(lay, start_id)
   787	        try:
   788	            lgs = logits_at(model, lay["ids"][:queries[-1]["pos"] + 1], [q["pos"] for q in queries])
   789	        except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   790	            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   791	            return {"pass": 0, "why": "oom"}
   792	        wrong = 0
   793	        for i, q in enumerate(queries):
   794	            _pred, margin, ok = pr_job.decision(lgs[i], q["true"])
   795	            if not ok or margin <= 0:
   796	                wrong += 1
   797	        del lgs
   798	        pin_wrong = sum(not r["ok"] for r in pins)
   799	        covered = kind == "covering"
   800	        # The pin reads every row. A covering run also queries every row inside the delay-16 tape.
   801	        return {"pass": int(pin_wrong == 0 and wrong == 0), "why": kind, "pin_wrong": pin_wrong,
   802	                "run_wrong": wrong, "n": len(queries), "covered_in_16": int(covered), "tokens": lay["n"]}
   803	    # bare
   804	    cover = covering_or_none(lambda s, e: pr_data.DELTA[(s, e)], pr_data.STATES, pr_data.EVENTS, "s0", GATE_LEN)
   805	    try:
   806	        pins = pr_job.pin_rows(model, pi, rows, bare["id_of"])
   807	    except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   808	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   809	        return {"pass": 0, "why": "oom"}
   810	    ev = pr_data.events_for_delay(GATE_LEN)
   811	    queries = _queries_bare(len(pi), ev, bare["id_of"], lambda s, e: pr_data.DELTA[(s, e)], "s0")
   812	    ids = bare_ids(pi, ev, bare["id_of"], lambda s, e: pr_data.DELTA[(s, e)], "s0")
   813	    try:
   814	        lgs = logits_at(model, ids, [q["pos"] for q in queries])
   815	    except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   816	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   817	        return {"pass": 0, "why": "oom"}
   818	    wrong = 0
   819	    for i, q in enumerate(queries):
   820	        _pred, margin, ok = pr_job.decision(lgs[i], q["true"])
   821	        if not ok or margin <= 0:
   822	            wrong += 1
   823	    del lgs
   824	    pin_wrong = sum(not r["ok"] for r in pins)
   825	    return {"pass": int(pin_wrong == 0 and wrong == 0), "why": "pin+delay16" if cover is None else "covering",
   826	            "pin_wrong": pin_wrong, "run_wrong": wrong, "n": len(queries),
   827	            "covered_in_16": 0 if cover is None else 1, "tokens": len(ids)}
   828	
   829	
   830	def _run_draw(model, state, out, fmt, k, otok, tok, pool, max_pos, t0, limit, persist):
   831	    rec = next(d for d in state["draws"] if d["k"] == k)
   832	    if fmt == "bare":
   833	        bare = pr_job._table_from_pool(pool, tok, k, int(state["seed"]))
   834	        rows = pr_data.rows_state_major()
   835	        pi, _spans = pr_data.encode_rows(rows, bare["id_of"], bare["nl"])
   836	        transit = lambda s, e: pr_data.DELTA[(s, e)]
   837	        tau_max = bare_tau_max(max_pos, len(pi))
   838	        held = pr_data.HELD
   839	        id_of = bare["id_of"]
   840	        pin = Pin(model, pi)
   841	
   842	        def events_of(tau):
   843	            return pr_data.events_for_delay(tau)
   844	
   845	        def queries_of(events):
   846	            return _queries_bare(len(pi), events, id_of, transit, "s0")
   847	
   848	        def ids_of(events):
   849	            return bare_ids(pi, events, id_of, transit, "s0")
   850	
   851	        held_state, held_event = id_of["q"], id_of["star"]
   852	        dest_of = {(id_of[s], id_of[e]): id_of[t] for s, e, t in rows}
   853	        start_state = id_of["s0"]
   854	        kind = "bare"
   855	        stride = BARE_STRIDE
   856	        states, events_abc, start = pr_data.STATES, pr_data.EVENTS, "s0"
   857	    else:
   858	        trained = draw_trained(otok, k, int(state["seed"]))
   859	        if "error" in trained:
   860	            state["cells"][f"{fmt}|{k}|build"] = {"error": trained["error"]}
   861	            persist()
   862	            return
   863	        transit = trained["transit"]
   864	        held = trained["held"]
   865	        states, events_abc, start = trained["states"], trained["events"], trained["start"]
   866	
   867	        def measure(tau, _tr=trained):
   868	            ev = first_use_events(_tr["transit"], _tr["states"], _tr["events"], _tr["start"], tau, _tr["held"])
   869	            if ev is None:
   870	                return False
   871	            made, _why = _tr["make"](ev)
   872	            return made is not None and made["lay"]["n"] <= max_pos
   873	
   874	        tau_max = fit_even_tau(measure, 16, max_pos)
   875	        # program pin from a short tape
   876	        ev16 = first_use_events(transit, states, events_abc, start, 16, held)
   877	        made16, why = trained["make"](ev16)
   878	        if made16 is None:
   879	            state["cells"][f"{fmt}|{k}|build"] = {"error": why}
   880	            persist()
   881	            return
   882	        lay16 = made16["lay"]
   883	        program = lay16["ids"][lay16["prog_start"]:lay16["prog_start"] + lay16["prog_len"]]
   884	        pin = Pin(model, program)
   885	        reg = made16["lit"]["registers"][0]
   886	        start_id = pb_lit.name_id(otok, reg["start"])
   887	        held_state = pb_lit.name_id(otok, trained["held_names"][0])
   888	        held_event = pb_lit.name_id(otok, trained["held_names"][1])
   889	        dest_of = {(pb_lit.name_id(otok, s), pb_lit.name_id(otok, e)): pb_lit.name_id(otok, t)
   890	                   for s, e, t in reg["rows"]}
   891	        start_state = start_id
   892	        kind = "trained"
   893	        stride = 2
   894	
   895	        def events_of(tau, _tr=trained):
   896	            return first_use_events(_tr["transit"], _tr["states"], _tr["events"], _tr["start"], tau, _tr["held"])
   897	
   898	        def queries_of(events, _tr=trained):
   899	            made, why = _tr["make"](events)
   900	            if made is None:
   901	                raise RuntimeError(why)
   902	            return _queries_trained(made["lay"], start_id), made
   903	
   904	        def ids_of(events):
   905	            _q, made = queries_of(events)
   906	            return made["lay"]["ids"], made
   907	
   908	    taus = ladder_for(tau_max)
   909	    state.setdefault("tau", {}).setdefault(fmt, {})[str(k)] = {"tau_max": tau_max, "ladder": taus, "stride": stride}
   910	    persist()
   911	
   912	    def one_cell(arm, tau):
   913	        key = f"{fmt}|{k}|{arm}|{tau}"
   914	        if key in state["cells"]:
   915	            return False
   916	        if _overdue(t0, limit):
   917	            state["budget_stop"] = key
   918	            persist()
   919	            return True
   920	        print(json.dumps({"cell": key}), flush=True)
   921	        t1 = time.perf_counter()
   922	        ev = events_of(tau)
   923	        if fmt == "trained":
   924	            queries, made = queries_of(ev)
   925	            ids = made["lay"]["ids"]
   926	            run_start = made["lay"]["run_start"]
   927	        else:
   928	            queries = queries_of(ev)
   929	            ids = ids_of(ev)
   930	            run_start = len(pi)
   931	        _mark_held(queries, held_state, held_event, tau)
   932	        name = f"{fmt}-d{k}-{arm}-t{tau}.json"
   933	        if arm == "tf":
   934	            # The logit is read at the event. Tokens after that event are not required.
   935	            fed = ids[:queries[-1]["pos"] + 1]
   936	            scored, err = _score_tf(model, fed, queries, pin, tau, out, name)
   937	        else:
   938	            import pb_pin as _pb
   939	            past = _pb.prefill(model, ids[:run_start])
   940	            scored, err = _free_run(model, past, run_start, queries, pin, tau, (held_state, held_event),
   941	                                    dest_of, start_state, kind, out, name, t0, limit)
   942	        cell = {"seconds": round(time.perf_counter() - t1, 3), "error": err}
   943	        if scored:
   944	            cell.update(scored)
   945	        state["cells"][key] = cell
   946	        persist()
   947	        return err == "budget"
   948	
   949	    def corrupt():
   950	        # Short distances first, then the largest gap that fits. Returns True when the budget is spent.
   951	        targets = list(CORRUPT_TARGETS)
   952	        g_max = _largest_gap(fmt, max_pos, transit, states, events_abc, start, held, stride,
   953	                             None if fmt == "bare" else trained, len(pi) if fmt == "bare" else None)
   954	        if g_max is not None and g_max >= 2:
   955	            dist = token_distance(g_max, stride)
   956	            if dist not in [token_distance(gap_for(t, stride), stride) for t in targets]:
   957	                targets.append(dist)
   958	        for target in targets:
   959	            if _overdue(t0, limit):
   960	                state["budget_stop"] = f"{fmt} d{k} corruption {target}"
   961	                persist()
   962	                return True
   963	            g = gap_for(target, stride) if target in CORRUPT_TARGETS else g_max
   964	            key = f"{fmt}|{k}|corr|{g}"
   965	            if any(c.get("key") == key for c in state["corruption"]):
   966	                continue
   967	            built = corruption_events(transit, states, events_abc, start, held, g)
   968	            if built is None:
   969	                state["corruption"].append({"key": key, "error": "no tape", "target": target})
   970	                persist()
   971	                continue
   972	            ev, first = built
   973	            if fmt == "trained":
   974	                made, why = trained["make"](ev)
   975	                if made is None or made["lay"]["n"] > max_pos:
   976	                    state["corruption"].append({"key": key, "error": why or "context", "target": target})
   977	                    persist()
   978	                    continue
   979	                queries = _queries_trained(made["lay"], start_id)
   980	                ids = made["lay"]["ids"]
   981	                out_pos = made["lay"]["run_start"] + first * stride + 1
   982	            else:
   983	                queries = queries_of(ev)
   984	                ids = ids_of(ev)
   985	                # The bare destination is written as the next step's state token.
   986	                out_pos = queries[first + 1]["state_pos"]
   987	            reuse = queries[first + g]
   988	            if reuse["state_pos"] == out_pos:
   989	                state["corruption"].append({"key": key, "error": "overwrite collides with the reuse", "target": target})
   990	                persist()
   991	                continue
   992	            true_out = ids[out_pos]
   993	            if fmt == "trained":
   994	                cands = [i for i in made["lay"]["states"][0] if i != true_out]
   995	            else:
   996	                cands = [id_of[s] for s in pr_data.STATES if id_of[s] != true_out]
   997	            wrong = cands[0]
   998	            dirty = list(ids)
   999	            dirty[out_pos] = wrong
  1000	            fed_c = ids[:reuse["pos"] + 1]
  1001	            fed_d = dirty[:reuse["pos"] + 1]
  1002	            try:
  1003	                lg_c = logits_at(model, fed_c, [reuse["pos"]])[0]
  1004	                lg_d = logits_at(model, fed_d, [reuse["pos"]])[0]
  1005	            except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
  1006	                import torch
  1007	                torch.cuda.empty_cache()
  1008	                state["corruption"].append({"key": key, "error": "oom", "target": target, "g": g})
  1009	                persist()
  1010	                continue
  1011	            _pc, mc, oc = pr_job.decision(lg_c, reuse["true"])
  1012	            pd, md, _od = pr_job.decision(lg_d, reuse["true"])
  1013	            del lg_c, lg_d
  1014	            follow_t = int(pd == reuse["true"])
  1015	            follow_h = int(pd == wrong)
  1016	            state["corruption"].append({
  1017	                "key": key, "draw": k, "format": fmt, "target": int(target), "g": int(g),
  1018	                "distance": int(reuse["pos"] - out_pos), "first": int(first),
  1019	                "control_ok": int(oc), "control_margin": round(float(mc), 4),
  1020	                "follow_t": follow_t, "follow_h": follow_h,
  1021	                "neither": int(follow_t == 0 and follow_h == 0),
  1022	                "corrupt_margin": round(float(md), 4),
  1023	            })
  1024	            persist()
  1025	            print(json.dumps({"corruption": key, "distance": int(reuse["pos"] - out_pos),
  1026	                              "follow_h": follow_h, "follow_t": follow_t, "control_ok": int(oc)}), flush=True)
  1027	        return False
  1028	
  1029	    # Teacher-forced exposure first, then corruption, then free-runs. A budget stop keeps the earlier cells.
  1030	    for tau in taus:
  1031	        if one_cell("tf", tau):
  1032	            return
  1033	    if corrupt():
  1034	        return
  1035	    for tau in taus:
  1036	        if one_cell("free", tau):
  1037	            return
  1038	
  1039	
  1040	def _largest_gap(fmt, max_pos, transit, states, events, start, held, stride, trained, pi_len):
  1041	    if fmt == "bare":
  1042	        probe = corruption_events(transit, states, events, start, held, 2)
  1043	        if probe is None:
  1044	            return None
  1045	        _ev, first = probe
  1046	        hi = (max_pos - pi_len) // stride - first - 1
  1047	        lo, best = 2, None
  1048	        while lo <= hi:
  1049	            mid = (lo + hi) // 2
  1050	            ev = corruption_events(transit, states, events, start, held, mid)
  1051	            if ev is not None and pi_len + stride * (ev[1] + mid + 1) <= max_pos:
  1052	                best = mid
  1053	                lo = mid + 1
  1054	            else:
  1055	                hi = mid - 1
  1056	        return best
  1057	    lo, hi = 2, max_pos
  1058	    best = None
  1059	    while lo <= hi:
  1060	        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
  1061	        ev = corruption_events(transit, states, events, start, held, mid)
  1062	        if ev is None:
  1063	            hi = mid - 1
  1064	            continue
  1065	        made, _why = trained["make"](ev[0])
  1066	        if made is not None and made["lay"]["n"] <= max_pos:
  1067	            best = mid
  1068	            lo = mid + 1
  1069	        else:
  1070	            hi = mid - 1
  1071	    return best
  1072	
  1073	
  1074	def _median(xs):
  1075	    xs = sorted(xs)
  1076	    n = len(xs)
  1077	    if n == 0:
  1078	        return None
  1079	    if n % 2:
  1080	        return xs[n // 2]
  1081	    return (xs[n // 2 - 1] + xs[n // 2]) / 2
  1082	
  1083	
  1084	def report_md(path):
  1085	    state = json.loads((pathlib.Path(path) / "runs.json").read_text())
  1086	    lines = ["## P2-v2 results", "", "Unaudited. An independent audit follows before anyone believes them.", ""]
  1087	    host = state.get("host") or {}
  1088	    lines.append(f"Job `p2v2-savant-r3`, seed {state.get('seed')}, host {host.get('host')}, "
  1089	                 f"gpu {host.get('gpu')}.")
  1090	    lines.append(f"CPU {host.get('cpu')}.")
  1091	    cfg = state.get("config") or {}
  1092	    lines.append(f"max_position_embeddings {state.get('max_position_embeddings')}. "
  1093	                 f"Sliding window {cfg.get('sliding_window')}.")
  1094	    adapter = state.get("adapter") or {}
  1095	    if adapter.get("sha256"):
  1096	        lines.append(f"Adapter sha256 {adapter['sha256']}.")
  1097	    lines.append(f"Complete {state.get('complete')}. Budget stop {state.get('budget_stop')}. "
  1098	                 f"Seconds {state.get('seconds')}.")
  1099	    lines.append(f"Dropped formats {state.get('dropped')}. Passing draws {state.get('passing')}.")
  1100	    lines.append("")
  1101	    for draw in state.get("draws") or []:
  1102	        lines.append(f"- draw {draw['k']}: gate {json.dumps(draw.get('gate'), sort_keys=True)}")
  1103	    lines.append("")
  1104	    lines.append("First-use margin by format, arm, draw and τ. A missing free-run margin diverged before exposure.")
  1105	    cells = state.get("cells") or {}
  1106	    for key in sorted(cells):
  1107	        cell = cells[key]
  1108	        first = cell.get("first")
  1109	        lines.append(f"- {key}: exposed {cell.get('exposed')} margin {None if not first else first.get('margin')} "
  1110	                     f"ok {None if not first else first.get('ok')} errors {cell.get('n_err')} of {cell.get('n')} "
  1111	                     f"pin errors {cell.get('pin_err')} of {cell.get('pin_n')} "
  1112	                     f"diverged {cell.get('diverged_before_exposure')} error {cell.get('error')} "
  1113	                     f"seconds {cell.get('seconds')}.")
  1114	    lines.append("")
  1115	    for fmt in ("trained", "bare"):
  1116	        if fmt in (state.get("dropped") or []):
  1117	            lines.append(f"{fmt}: out of distribution at delay 16, dropped from the long-delay analysis.")
  1118	            continue
  1119	        points = []
  1120	        taus = []
  1121	        info = (state.get("tau") or {}).get(fmt) or {}
  1122	        for k, rec in info.items():
  1123	            taus = rec.get("ladder") or taus
  1124	        for tau in taus:
  1125	            margins = []
  1126	            for k in (state.get("passing") or {}).get(fmt) or []:
  1127	                cell = cells.get(f"{fmt}|{k}|tf|{tau}") or {}
  1128	                first = cell.get("first")
  1129	                if first and first.get("margin") is not None and first.get("step") == tau:
  1130	                    margins.append(first["margin"])
  1131	            if margins:
  1132	                points.append((tau, _median(margins), len(margins)))
  1133	        lines.append(f"{fmt} teacher-forced median first-use margin: {points}.")
  1134	        if len(points) < 2:
  1135	            lines.append(f"{fmt} fall: untested.")
  1136	        elif any(b[1] >= a[1] for a, b in zip(points, points[1:])):
  1137	            lines.append(f"{fmt} fall: flat or rising. Falsifier fires on the trained format." if fmt == "trained"
  1138	                         else f"{fmt} fall: flat or rising.")
  1139	        else:
  1140	            lines.append(f"{fmt} fall: each longer τ has a smaller median margin.")
  1141	    lines.append("")
  1142	    corr = state.get("corruption") or []
  1143	    lines.append("Corruption. follow_h copies the overwritten destination; follow_t follows the table; "
  1144	                 "control_ok is the uncorrupted twin.")
  1145	    for row in corr:
  1146	        lines.append(f"- {json.dumps(row, sort_keys=True)}")
  1147	    by = {}
  1148	    for row in corr:
  1149	        if row.get("follow_h") is None:
  1150	            continue
  1151	        by.setdefault((row["format"], row["distance"]), []).append(row)
  1152	    for key, rows in sorted(by.items(), key=lambda kv: (kv[0][0], kv[0][1])):
  1153	        n = len(rows)
  1154	        copies = sum(r["follow_h"] == 1 for r in rows)
  1155	        errs = sum(r["control_ok"] == 0 for r in rows)
  1156	        lines.append(f"{key[0]} distance {key[1]}: copies {copies} of {n}, uncorrupted errors {errs} of {n}.")
  1157	    if not any(r.get("follow_h") == 1 for r in corr):
  1158	        lines.append("Corrupted history was not copied at any recorded distance.")
  1159	    else:
  1160	        lines.append("Corrupted history was copied at least once.")
  1161	    lines.append("")
  1162	    pin_n = sum(int(c.get("pin_n") or 0) for c in cells.values())
  1163	    pin_err = sum(int(c.get("pin_err") or 0) for c in cells.values())
  1164	    lines.append(f"Pinned twin on full-history errors: wrong {pin_err} of {pin_n}.")
  1165	    if pin_n == 0:
  1166	        lines.append("Pin prediction untested: the full-history runs recorded no errors.")
  1167	    elif pin_err == 0:
  1168	        lines.append("The pinned twin is correct on every recorded full-history error.")
  1169	    elif pin_err == pin_n:
  1170	        lines.append("The pinned twin errs on every recorded full-history error. Falsifier fires.")
  1171	    else:
  1172	        lines.append("The pinned twin is correct on some recorded errors and wrong on others.")
  1173	    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
  1174	
  1175

  1177	    import argparse

  1179	    ap.add_argument("--selftest", action="store_true")
  1180	    ap.add_argument("--report", default="")

  1188	        return

   246	## P2-v2 registration
   247	
   248	Registered before any P2-v2 spend. P1 and P3 are not rerun. The job is `p2v2-savant-r3` in `pr_v2.py`: Savant r3 only, seed 20261028, adapter `/arvol/arith-s1r2/adapter_step500`. One A10G job, `max_seconds` 28723. `pr_modal.reservation` of that limit is $14.999818, the largest integer limit at or under $15. The ledger has $1.8692 settled, so the reservation also fits the $25 cap.
   249	
   250	The trained format is a `pb_families.cycle` program (train split, one register, at most 16 rows) laid out by `br_core.layout` and `pb_lit`. Those builders are imported. The tape is the only part constructed here, so that one held row is first used at a chosen event index. The bare format is the 16-row machine in `pr_data.py`: `encode_rows`, then a stream of `(state, event)`.
   251	
   252	Draws are seeds k = 0, 1, … until a format has four passing draws or k reaches 16. The delay-16 gate is a pin of every row, with margin above 0, and a teacher-forced run of 16 events that is correct at every step. Trained Π is the program, without the chat prompt, positions restarted. Bare Π is the encoded table. The bare graph has no covering walk of length 16, so its 16-event run is `events_for_delay(16)` and the pin is what reads every row. A format with no passing draw is out of distribution and is dropped from the long-delay analysis.
   253	
   254	τ is 16, 256, 2048, 8192, and τ_max. τ_max is the largest even event index, at least 16, that still fits `max_position_embeddings` read from the config, with four later reuses of the held row. It is computed per draw. For the bare table the fit is `pi_len + 2 * (τ + 1 + 8) ≤ max_position_embeddings`; on a 40960 window and a 64-token table that is 20438. For the trained format the prompt contains the tape, so the fit is `br_core.layout`'s token length. A fixed τ larger than that draw's τ_max is dropped, and τ_max is added when it is not already in the list.
   255	
   256	Each τ has two arms. Teacher forcing writes the true states, so the held row is exposed. Free running writes the model's prediction. An error at an event index strictly before τ is "diverged before exposure" and that arm has no first-use margin. The step log is `(step, event-token position, state written, event, prediction, true destination, margin)`, plus the pin bit on errors. The margin is positive when the true destination is the argmax.
   257	
   258	At every full-history error the same `(state, event)` is read with `pb_pin.prefill` and `pb_pin.tick`. A pair is forwarded once; the pin does not depend on the run. The trained pin's Π is the program. The bare pin's Π is the table.
   259	
   260	The corruption test replaces the swap test. On a teacher-forced trajectory one earlier destination of the held row is overwritten with a different state token. The next use is g ≥ 2 events later, so the overwritten token is not the state token of that reuse. The token distance is `g * stride − 1`. Both formats have stride 2, so the aims 64, 512 and 4096 are the distances 65, 513 and 4097. The largest g that fits is included when its distance is not already one of those. The uncorrupted twin is scored at the same reuse. `follow_h` is the overwritten token, `follow_t` is the table's destination, and neither is the rest.
   261	
   262	The fall is the median, across the passing draws, of the teacher-forced first-use margin at each exposed τ. It falls when every longer τ has a strictly smaller median. The flat-or-rising falsifier is that comparison in the trained format. Fewer than two τ is untested. Free-run margins are reported and are not that falsifier.
   263	
   264	At the shortest recorded corruption distance, the copy rate is the fraction of draws with `follow_h` 1 and the control error rate is the fraction whose uncorrupted twin is wrong. The copy claim holds when the copy rate is higher. The falsifier fires when `follow_h` is 0 at every recorded distance. The pin claim holds when the pin is correct on every recorded full-history error. Its falsifier fires when the pin is wrong on every one of them. No errors leaves the pin claim untested.
   265	
   266	Work order is the gate, then for each passing draw the teacher-forced ladder, the corruption distances, and the free-runs, trained format before bare. The process stops 90 seconds before `max_seconds` and keeps the cells already written. Step logs are separate json files. The summary is `runs.json`. These results stay unaudited.
   267
