   356	- `{"control_margin": 13.875, "control_ok": 1, "corrupt_margin": 3.75, "distance": 65, "draw": 3, "first": 1, "follow_h": 0, "follow_t": 1, "format": "trained", "g": 33, "key": "trained|3|corr|33", "neither": 0, "target": 64}`
   357	- `{"control_margin": 11.75, "control_ok": 1, "corrupt_margin": 4.0, "distance": 513, "draw": 3, "first": 1, "follow_h": 0, "follow_t": 1, "format": "trained", "g": 257, "key": "trained|3|corr|257", "neither": 0, "target": 512}`
   358	- `{"control_margin": 11.5, "control_ok": 1, "corrupt_margin": 3.75, "distance": 4097, "draw": 3, "first": 1, "follow_h": 0, "follow_t": 1, "format": "trained", "g": 2049, "key": "trained|3|corr|2049", "neither": 0, "target": 4096}`
   359	- `{"control_margin": 8.875, "control_ok": 1, "corrupt_margin": 4.625, "distance": 20399, "draw": 3, "first": 1, "follow_h": 0, "follow_t": 1, "format": "trained", "g": 10200, "key": "trained|3|corr|10200", "neither": 0, "target": 20399}`
   360	trained distance 65: copies 0 of 4, uncorrupted errors 0 of 4 (median margin 15.0625 uncorrupted vs 8.1875 corrupted).
   361	trained distance 513: copies 0 of 4, uncorrupted errors 0 of 4 (median margin 13.625 uncorrupted vs 8.6875 corrupted).
   362	trained distance 4097: copies 0 of 4, uncorrupted errors 0 of 4 (median margin 12.9375 uncorrupted vs 8.125 corrupted).
   363	trained distance 20383: copies 0 of 1, uncorrupted errors 0 of 1.
   364	trained distance 20399: copies 0 of 2, uncorrupted errors 0 of 2.
   365	trained distance 20409: copies 0 of 1, uncorrupted errors 0 of 1.
   366	Corrupted history was not copied at any recorded distance (`follow_h` 0 of 16, `follow_t` 16 of 16). Copy claim at distance 65 (0 of 4 vs 0 of 4): fails. Corruption falsifier fires.
   367	
   368	Pinned twin on full-history errors: wrong 0 of 3 (`trained|0|tf|8192` step 8196 pos 32913 margin -0.125, pin ok 1; `trained|2|tf|10190` step 10186 pos 40936 margin -1.375, pin ok 1; `trained|2|free|10190` step 10186 pos 40936 margin -1.0, pin ok 1).
   369	The pinned twin is correct on every recorded full-history error. Pin claim: holds. Pin falsifier: does not fire.
   370	
     1	"""
     2	BRIDGE core: plain-mode examples with their token layout, model loading, and the hooks the anatomy needs.
     3	
     4	A plain-mode sequence (PROMPTBOX §3.6, pb_loop plain mode) is
     5	
     6	    prompt (chat template, question with the tape)  |  program (literal table Π, ends `<exec/>`)  |  `\\n<run>\\n`
     7	    |  run: e_1 s_1 [s_1' …] e_2 s_2 …  |  `\\n</run>\\n`
     8	
     9	where the program is the true literal program (`pb_lit.program_ids`, the "oracle" program: PROMPTBOX's model writes
    10	exactly this table in 98.7% of gate cases), and the run is the true trace. The model writes the run itself in plain
    11	mode: it copies each event from the prompt (reading) and writes each register's next value (stepping).
    12	
    13	Teacher forcing on the true sequence gives, at every run token, the model's greedy choice given a correct history.
    14	Greedy free-running decoding equals the true run if and only if every one of those choices is right (induction on
    15	the position), so one forward pass decides whether the greedy run of that input is exact, up to floating-point
    16	differences between the two code paths at near-ties (measured by br_free.py).
    17	
    18	Torch is imported only inside functions that need it.
    19	"""
    20	
    21	from __future__ import annotations
    22	
    23	import pathlib
    24	import random
    25	import sys
    26	
    27	for _p in ("/root/pb", str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "promptbox")):
    28	    if pathlib.Path(_p).exists() and _p not in sys.path:
    29	        sys.path.insert(0, _p)
    30	
    31	import pb_lit  # noqa: E402
    32	from pb_data import family_example  # noqa: E402
    33	
    34	MODEL = "Qwen/Qwen3-4B"
    35	SAVANT = "/pb/rlg-ours/adapter_iter60"
    36	TRAINED = ("parity", "cups", "robot", "compass", "cycle", "prose")
    37	CONTEXT = 40960
    38	
    39	
    40	def _dev():
    41	    import torch
    42	    return "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    43	
    44	
    45	def enc(tok, s):
    46	    return list(tok.encode(s, add_special_tokens=False).ids)
    47	
    48	
    49	def tokenizer(model=MODEL):
    50	    import glob
    51	    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
    52	    local = glob.glob(str(pathlib.Path.home() / ".cache/huggingface/hub/models--Qwen--Qwen3-4B/snapshots/*/tokenizer.json"))
    53	    if local:
    54	        return Tokenizer.from_file(local[0])
    55	    from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download
    56	    return Tokenizer.from_file(str(pathlib.Path(snapshot_download(model)) / "tokenizer.json"))
    57	
    58	
    59	def _tape_positions(tok, prompt, question, lit):
    60	    """Token indices (in the encoded prompt) of the tape's events, by the clock's own walk (pb_lit.walk)."""
    61	    q0 = prompt.find(question)
    62	    assert q0 >= 0
    63	    a, b = pb_lit.find_span(question, *lit["anchors"])
    64	    a, b = a + q0, b + q0
    65	    e = tok.encode(prompt, add_special_tokens=False)
    66	    names = set(lit["events"])
    67	    out = []
    68	    for i, (s, t) in enumerate(e.offsets):
    69	        raw = prompt[s:t]
    70	        s2, t2 = s + len(raw) - len(raw.lstrip()), t - (len(raw) - len(raw.rstrip()))
    71	        if t2 <= a or s2 >= b:
    72	            continue
    73	        if prompt[s2:t2] in names:
    74	            out.append(i)
    75	    return out
    76	
    77	
    78	def layout(tok, ex, lit):
    79	    """ids of the whole plain-mode sequence and the index of every part. Run step k (0-based) has its event at
    80	    run_start + k*stride and register r's value at run_start + k*stride + 1 + r."""
    81	    prompt = enc(tok, ex["prompt"])
    82	    prog = pb_lit.program_ids(tok, lit)
    83	    # Π positions, rebuilt piece by piece exactly as pb_lit.program_ids builds the ids
    84	    nl = enc(tok, "\n")
    85	    import json as _json
    86	    head = enc(tok, f"<program>\ntask: {lit['task']}\ninput: {lit['input']}\n")
    87	    before, after = lit["anchors"]
    88	    head += enc(tok, "tape: after " + _json.dumps(before, ensure_ascii=False) + " until "
    89	                + _json.dumps(after, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    90	    pos = len(prompt) + len(head)
    91	    rows, rebuilt = [], list(head)
    92	    for r in lit["registers"]:
    93	        reg_head = enc(tok, f"register {r['name']}: start") + [pb_lit.name_id(tok, r["start"])] + nl
    94	        rebuilt += reg_head
    95	        pos += len(reg_head)
    96	        rr = []
    97	        for s, e, t in r["rows"]:
    98	            ids = [pb_lit.name_id(tok, s), pb_lit.name_id(tok, e), pb_lit.name_id(tok, t)]
    99	            rr.append({"pos": pos, "s": ids[0], "e": ids[1], "t": ids[2]})
   100	            rebuilt += ids + nl
   101	            pos += 4
   102	        rows.append(rr)
   103	    rebuilt += enc(tok, f"answer: {lit['answer_line']}\n</program>\n<exec/>")
   104	    assert rebuilt == prog, "program layout does not match pb_lit.program_ids"
   105	    opener = enc(tok, "\n<run>\n")
   106	    run = pb_lit.run_ids(tok, lit)
   107	    closer = enc(tok, "\n</run>\n")
   108	    ids = prompt + prog + opener + run + closer
   109	    R = len(lit["registers"])
   110	    tape_pos = _tape_positions(tok, ex["prompt"], ex["question"], lit)
   111	    reg_states = [sorted({pb_lit.name_id(tok, v) for v in r["name_of"].values()}) for r in lit["registers"]]
   112	    events = sorted({pb_lit.name_id(tok, e) for e in lit["events"]})
   113	    table = [{(x["s"], x["e"]): x["t"] for x in rr} for rr in rows]
   114	    starts = [pb_lit.name_id(tok, r["start"]) for r in lit["registers"]]
   115	    return {"ids": ids, "prompt_len": len(prompt), "prog_start": len(prompt), "prog_len": len(prog),
   116	            "run_start": len(prompt) + len(prog) + len(opener), "T": len(lit["tape"]), "R": R, "stride": 1 + R,
   117	            "rows": rows, "tape_pos": tape_pos, "states": reg_states, "events": events, "table": table,
   118	            "starts": starts, "n": len(ids)}
   119	
   120	
   121	def run_tokens(lay):
   122	    """(index, kind, step, register) for every run token; kind 'e' (read) or 's' (step)."""
   123	    out = []
   124	    for k in range(lay["T"]):
   125	        p = lay["run_start"] + k * lay["stride"]
   126	        out.append((p, "e", k, -1))
   127	        for r in range(lay["R"]):
   128	            out.append((p + 1 + r, "s", k, r))
   129	    return out
   130	
   131	
   132	def examples(tok, families, lengths, n, seed, split="train", max_tokens=CONTEXT - 64):
   133	    """n examples per (family, length), drawn as pb_loop draws its gate cells; skipped if unpinnable or over context.
   134	    Each cell has its own generator, seeded by (seed, family, length), so a cell's examples do not depend on which
   135	    other cells a job draws."""
   136	    out, skipped = [], {}
   137	    for fam in families:
   138	        for L in lengths:
   139	            rng = random.Random(f"{seed}|{fam}|{L}")
   140	            made, tries = 0, 0
   141	            while made < n and tries < 20 * n:
   142	                tries += 1
   143	                ex = family_example(rng, fam, L=L, split=split)
   144	                lit, why = pb_lit.literal(ex, tok)
   145	                if lit is None:
   146	                    skipped[f"{fam}|L{L}"] = skipped.get(f"{fam}|L{L}", 0) + 1
   147	                    continue
   148	                lay = layout(tok, ex, lit)
   149	                if lay["n"] > max_tokens:
   150	                    skipped[f"{fam}|L{L}|over_context"] = skipped.get(f"{fam}|L{L}|over_context", 0) + 1
   151	                    continue
   152	                assert len(lay["tape_pos"]) == lay["T"], (fam, L, len(lay["tape_pos"]), lay["T"])
   153	                out.append({"family": fam, "L": L, "cell": f"{fam}|L{L}", "ex": ex, "lit": lit, "lay": lay})
   154	                made += 1
   155	    return out, skipped
   156	
   157	
   158	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ model
   159	
   160	def load_model(adapter=SAVANT, model=MODEL):
   161	    """bf16 on the GPU, adapter merged in memory into a fresh copy (never a cached merge: PROMPTBOX §3.10)."""
   162	    import torch
   163	    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM
   164	    m = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model, dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation="sdpa").to(_dev())
   165	    if adapter:
   166	        from peft import PeftModel
   167	        m = PeftModel.from_pretrained(m, adapter).merge_and_unload()
   168	    m.eval()
   169	    for p in m.parameters():
   170	        p.requires_grad_(False)
   171	    return m
   172	
   173	
   174	def hidden(model, ids=None, embeds=None):
   175	    """Final-normed hidden states (n, d) of one causal sequence."""
   176	    import torch
   177	    kw = {"input_ids": torch.tensor([ids], device=_dev())} if embeds is None else {"inputs_embeds": embeds[None]}
   178	    return model.model(**kw, use_cache=False).last_hidden_state[0]
   179	
   180	
   181	def logits_at(model, h, positions, chunk=1024):
   182	    """Full-vocabulary logits (float32) of the next token at the given positions (small position sets only)."""
   183	    import torch
   184	    out = []
   185	    for i in range(0, len(positions), chunk):
   186	        idx = torch.tensor(positions[i:i + chunk], device=h.device)
   187	        out.append(model.lm_head(h[idx]).float())
   188	    return torch.cat(out) if out else None
   189	
   190	
   191	def margins(lg, targets, cand=None):
   192	    """Per row: target logit minus the best other logit (full vocabulary), argmax, and the margin among `cand`
   193	    (a list of candidate id lists, one per row) if given."""
   194	    import torch
   195	    t = torch.as_tensor(targets, device=lg.device)
   196	    tl = lg.gather(1, t[:, None])[:, 0]
   197	    other = lg.scatter(1, t[:, None], float("-inf")).max(1).values
   198	    res = {"margin": (tl - other), "argmax": lg.argmax(1)}
   199	    if cand is not None:
   200	        width = max(len(c) for c in cand)
   201	        ci = torch.tensor([list(c) + [c[0]] * (width - len(c)) for c in cand], device=lg.device)
   202	        cl = lg.gather(1, ci)
   203	        cl = cl.masked_fill(ci == t[:, None], float("-inf"))
   204	        res["margin_cand"] = tl - cl.max(1).values
   205	    return res
   206	
   207	
   208	def chunked_margins(model, h, positions, targets, cand, chunk=512):
   209	    """margins() over many positions without holding all the logits: numpy arrays margin, margin_cand, argmax."""
   210	    import numpy as np
   211	    import torch
   212	    m, mc, am = [], [], []
   213	    with torch.no_grad():
   214	        for i in range(0, len(positions), chunk):
   215	            idx = torch.tensor(positions[i:i + chunk], device=h.device)
   216	            lg = model.lm_head(h[idx]).float()
   217	            r = margins(lg, targets[i:i + chunk], cand[i:i + chunk])
   218	            m.append(r["margin"].cpu().numpy())
   219	            mc.append(r["margin_cand"].cpu().numpy())
   220	            am.append(r["argmax"].cpu().numpy())
   221	            del lg, r
   222	    return np.concatenate(m), np.concatenate(mc), np.concatenate(am)
   223	
   224	
   225	class AttnProbe:
     1	"""
     2	Literal-table programs and span-quoted tapes for the pinned mode (DEMO_DESIGN §2.1, §2.4 R2, §2.6).
     3	
     4	A family question becomes this response (ordinary decoding):
     5	
     6	    <program>
     7	    task: which spot hides the coin after the swaps
     8	    input: the swaps in order
     9	    tape: after "Swaps, in order: " until ". Where is the coin"
    10	    register ball: start C
    11	     A AB B
    12	     A BC A
    13	     …                                   one row per (state, event): ` s e t` and a newline, each name one token
    14	    answer: The coin is under {ball}.
    15	    </program>
    16	    <exec/>
    17	
    18	Then the clock (pb_loop) takes over:
    19	- tape: the prompt's own tokens between the quoted prose (after the first quote, up to the second). Each token's
    20	  text, stripped, is either an event name
    21	  (the table's own id for that name goes on the tape) or a separator (empty, comma, semicolon, full stop);
    22	  anything else rejects the tape.
    23	- run: per register, Π = that register's row tokens exactly as written; one pinned tick per event (pb_pin).
    24	- it appends `<final> ball=B </final>`, and the model writes the answer in words.
    25	
    26	Plain decoding, the comparison mode: after `<exec/>` the harness appends `<run>` and the model writes the run
    27	itself, one event then each register's value per step, then `</run>` and the answer.
    28	
    29	Only literal rows: every register must be independent (it reads no other register), every value and event must
    30	have a one-token name, and the rows are what the program's rules give, written out. Families that need
    31	arithmetic over large ranges (counts, sums, remainders) are not pinnable here and keep the plain format.
    32	"""
    33	
    34	from __future__ import annotations
    35	
    36	import json
    37	import re
    38	
    39	from pb_lang import ProgramError, deps, parse_program, run_program, step_reg
    40	
    41	NUM_WORDS = ("zero one two three four five six seven eight nine ten eleven twelve thirteen fourteen fifteen sixteen "
    42	             "seventeen eighteen nineteen twenty").split()
    43	SEPARATORS = {"", ",", ";", "."}
    44	MAX_ROWS = 256
    45	
    46	
    47	def _one(tok, text):
    48	    ids = tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False).ids
    49	    return ids[0] if len(ids) == 1 else None
    50	
    51	
    52	def name_id(tok, name):
    53	    """The one-token id of a name: with a leading space if that is one token, else bare (digits, brackets)."""
    54	    return _one(tok, " " + name) or _one(tok, name)
    55	
    56	
    57	def _value_names(tok, reg):
    58	    """Numeric registers: bare digits when every value is at most 9, number words up to twenty otherwise.
    59	    Other registers: their own names, if each is one token."""
    60	    disp = [str(reg.disp(v)) for v in reg.values]
    61	    if all(d.isdigit() for d in disp):
    62	        nums = [int(d) for d in disp]
    63	        if max(nums) <= 9:
    64	            return [str(n) for n in nums]
    65	        if max(nums) < len(NUM_WORDS):
    66	            return [NUM_WORDS[n] for n in nums]
    67	        return None
    68	    if all(name_id(tok, d) for d in disp) and len({name_id(tok, d) for d in disp}) == len(disp):
    69	        return disp
    70	    return None
    71	
    72	
    73	def item_span(question, items):
    74	    """Character span of the items in the question: the first place where they occur in order with only
    75	    separators (spaces, commas, semicolons, newlines) between them. None if they do not."""
    76	    first = str(items[0])
    77	    for m in re.finditer(re.escape(first), question):
    78	        pos, ok, starts = m.end(), True, [m.start()]
    79	        for it in items[1:]:
    80	            it = str(it)
    81	            k = pos
    82	            while k < len(question) and question[k] in " ,;\n\t":
    83	                k += 1
    84	            if not question.startswith(it, k):
    85	                ok = False
    86	                break
    87	            starts.append(k)
    88	            pos = k + len(it)
    89	        if ok:
    90	            return m.start(), pos, starts
    91	    return None
    92	
    93	
    94	def anchors(question, items, min_words=3, max_chars=80):
    95	    """The prose just before the first item and just after the last, quoted exactly. Each quote starts at min_words
    96	    words and grows a word at a time: `before` until it occurs once in the question, `after` until its first
    97	    occurrence after the list's start is the list's end. An empty quote means the start or end of the question.
    98	    Item anchors (the first and last few items) were dropped after the Stage 2 pilot: lists repeat, and a miscopied
    99	    tail usually occurs earlier in the list."""
   100	    span = item_span(question, items)
   101	    if span is None:
   102	        return None
   103	    a, b, _ = span
   104	    pre, post = question[:a], question[b:]
   105	    before = "" if not pre.strip() else None
   106	    words = list(re.finditer(r"\S+\s*", pre))
   107	    for w in range(min(min_words, len(words)), len(words) + 1):
   108	        if before is not None:
   109	            break
   110	        cand = pre[words[-w].start():]
   111	        if len(cand) > max_chars:
   112	            return None
   113	        if question.count(cand) == 1:
   114	            before = cand
   115	    after = "" if not post.strip() else None
   116	    words = list(re.finditer(r"\s*\S+", post))
   117	    for w in range(min(min_words, len(words)), len(words) + 1):
   118	        if after is not None:
   119	            break
   120	        cand = post[:words[w - 1].end()]
   121	        if len(cand) > max_chars:
   122	            return None
   123	        if question.find(cand, a) == b:
   124	            after = cand
   125	    if before is None or after is None:
   126	        return None
   127	    return before, after
   128	
   129	
   130	def find_span(question, before, after):
   131	    """The clock's reading of a tape line: from the end of the first occurrence of `before` (or the question's start)
   132	    to the first occurrence of `after` after that (or the question's end)."""
   133	    start = 0
   134	    if before:
   135	        i = question.find(before)
   136	        if i < 0:
   137	            return None
   138	        start = i + len(before)
   139	    end = len(question)
   140	    if after:
   141	        j = question.find(after, start)
   142	        if j < 0:
   143	            return None
   144	        end = j
   145	    return start, end
   146	
   147	
   148	def walk(tok, question, span, event_names):
   149	    """Events in the span, in order, by the text of each prompt token (stripped). Returns (events, None) or
   150	    (None, reason)."""
   151	    enc = tok.encode(question, add_special_tokens=False)
   152	    a, b = span
   153	    names = set(event_names)
   154	    out = []
   155	    for (s, e) in enc.offsets:
   156	        raw = question[s:e]
   157	        s, e = s + len(raw) - len(raw.lstrip()), e - (len(raw) - len(raw.rstrip()))
   158	        if e <= a or s >= b:
   159	            continue
   160	        if s < a or e > b:
   161	            return None, f"token crosses the span edge at {s}"
   162	        text = question[s:e]
   163	        if text in names:
   164	            out.append(text)
   165	        elif text not in SEPARATORS:
   166	            return None, f"not an event or separator: {text!r}"
   167	    return out, None
   168	
   169	
   170	def literal(ex, tok):
   171	    """The literal-table program for a family example, or None with a reason."""
   172	    try:
   173	        prog = parse_program(ex["prog_text"])
   174	    except ProgramError as exc:
   175	        return None, f"program: {exc}"
   176	    if prog.other:
   177	        return None, "has an 'other' event class"
   178	    for r in prog.registers:
   179	        if deps(prog, r) not in ([r.name], []):
   180	            return None, f"register {r.name} reads another register"
   181	    events = prog.event_classes()
   182	    if not all(name_id(tok, e) for e in events) or len({name_id(tok, e) for e in events}) != len(events):
   183	        return None, "an event has no one-token name"
   184	    regs = []
   185	    for r in prog.registers:
   186	        names = _value_names(tok, r)
   187	        if names is None:
   188	            return None, f"register {r.name} has values without one-token names"
   189	        if len(r.values) * len(events) > MAX_ROWS:
   190	            return None, f"register {r.name} has {len(r.values) * len(events)} rows"
   191	        name_of = dict(zip(r.values, names))
   192	        rows = []
   193	        for v in r.values:
   194	            for e in events:
   195	                state = dict(prog.start)
   196	                state[r.name] = v
   197	                rows.append((name_of[v], e, name_of[step_reg(prog, r, state, e)]))
   198	        regs.append({"name": r.name, "start": name_of[prog.start[r.name]], "rows": rows, "name_of": name_of})
   199	    items = [prog.classify(it) for it in ex["items"]]
   200	    anc = anchors(ex["question"], ex["items"])
   201	    if anc is None:
   202	        return None, "items are not a separator-delimited list in the question"
   203	    span = find_span(ex["question"], *anc)
   204	    tape, why = walk(tok, ex["question"], span, events) if span else (None, "anchors not found")
   205	    if tape != [str(i) for i in items]:
   206	        return None, f"the clock would misread the tape ({why})"
   207	    steps, final = run_program(prog, ex["items"])
   208	    task = re.search(r"^task: (.*)$", ex["prog_text"], re.M).group(1)
   209	    inp = re.search(r"^input: (.*)$", ex["prog_text"], re.M).group(1)
   210	    ans = re.search(r"^answer: (.*)$", ex["prog_text"], re.M).group(1)
   211	    return {"task": task, "input": inp, "answer_line": ans, "events": events, "registers": regs, "tape": tape,
   212	            "anchors": anc, "final": {r["name"]: r["name_of"][final[r["name"]]] for r in regs},
   213	            "trace": [{r["name"]: r["name_of"][st[r["name"]]] for r in regs} for _, _, st in steps],
   214	            "answer": ex["answer"]}, None
   215	
   216	
   217	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ token sequences
   218	
   219	def _enc(tok, text):
   220	    return list(tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False).ids)
   221	
   222	
   223	def program_ids(tok, lit):
   224	    nl = _enc(tok, "\n")
   225	    ids = _enc(tok, f"<program>\ntask: {lit['task']}\ninput: {lit['input']}\n")
   226	    before, after = lit["anchors"]
   227	    ids += _enc(tok, "tape: after " + json.dumps(before, ensure_ascii=False) + " until "
   228	                + json.dumps(after, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
   229	    for r in lit["registers"]:
   230	        ids += _enc(tok, f"register {r['name']}: start") + [name_id(tok, r["start"])] + nl
   231	        for s, e, t in r["rows"]:
   232	            ids += [name_id(tok, s), name_id(tok, e), name_id(tok, t)] + nl
   233	    ids += _enc(tok, f"answer: {lit['answer_line']}\n</program>\n<exec/>")
   234	    return ids
   235	
   236	
   237	def final_ids(tok, lit, final=None):
   238	    final = final or lit["final"]
   239	    ids = _enc(tok, "\n<final>")
   240	    for r in lit["registers"]:
   241	        ids += _enc(tok, f" {r['name']}=") + [name_id(tok, final[r["name"]])]
   242	    return ids + _enc(tok, " </final>\n")
   243	
   244	
   245	def run_ids(tok, lit):
   246	    ids = []
   247	    for ev, st in zip(lit["tape"], lit["trace"]):
   248	        ids.append(name_id(tok, ev))
   249	        ids += [name_id(tok, st[r["name"]]) for r in lit["registers"]]
   250	    return ids
   251	
   252	
   253	def example_ids(tok, ex, lit, mode, im_end):
   254	    """(ids, labels) for prompt + response. Harness-written spans (`<final>…</final>`, `<run>` opener) are inputs,
   255	    not targets."""
   256	    prompt = _enc(tok, ex["prompt"])
   257	    prog = program_ids(tok, lit)
   258	    ids, labels = prompt + prog, [-100] * len(prompt) + prog
   259	    if mode == "pinned":
   260	        fin = final_ids(tok, lit)
   261	        ids += fin
   262	        labels += [-100] * len(fin)
   263	    else:
   264	        opener = _enc(tok, "\n<run>\n")
   265	        run = run_ids(tok, lit) + _enc(tok, "\n</run>\n")
   266	        ids += opener + run
   267	        labels += [-100] * len(opener) + run
   268	    ans = _enc(tok, lit["answer"]) + [im_end]
   269	    return ids + ans, labels + ans

     1	"""
     2	Prompt and stream construction for PREDICT. No torch. The machine, the delays and the
     3	swap geometry are fixed here so a local self-test can check them before any GPU job.
     4	
     5	    python research/predict/pr_data.py
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	# Roles. Names are filled from the tokenizer's held-out pool; the transition structure is not.
    11	STATES = ("s0", "s1", "s2", "q")
    12	EVENTS = ("loop", "enter", "back", "star")
    13	HELD = ("q", "star")
    14	# (q, star) goes to s2. The swap test changes that one destination to s0.
    15	HELD_DEST = "s2"
    16	SWAP_DEST = "s0"
    17	# Four-row table for the swap grid, held row first, so the value token sits near the start.
    18	SWAP_KEYS = (("q", "star"), ("s0", "loop"), ("s1", "loop"), ("s0", "enter"))
    19	# Events before the delayed first use. Length 9, ends in s0. See events_for_delay.
    20	PREAMBLE = ("loop", "loop", "loop", "loop", "enter", "back", "enter", "back", "back")
    21	REUSES = 4
    22	SEED = 20261028
    23	
    24	DELTA = {
    25	    ("s0", "loop"): "s1",
    26	    ("s1", "loop"): "s0",
    27	    ("s2", "loop"): "s2",
    28	    ("q", "loop"): "q",
    29	    ("s0", "enter"): "q",
    30	    ("s1", "enter"): "q",
    31	    ("s2", "enter"): "q",
    32	    ("q", "enter"): "q",
    33	    ("s0", "back"): "s0",
    34	    ("s1", "back"): "s1",
    35	    ("s2", "back"): "s2",
    36	    ("q", "back"): "s0",
    37	    ("s0", "star"): "s1",
    38	    ("s1", "star"): "s0",
    39	    ("s2", "star"): "s0",
    40	    ("q", "star"): HELD_DEST,
    41	}
    42	
    43	
    44	def rows_state_major():
    45	    """All 16 rows. The held row is placed first so its value token is near position 0."""
    46	    rest = [(s, e, DELTA[(s, e)]) for s in STATES for e in EVENTS if (s, e) != HELD]
    47	    return [(HELD[0], HELD[1], HELD_DEST)] + rest
    48	
    49	
    50	def swap_rows():
    51	    return [(s, e, DELTA[(s, e)]) for s, e in SWAP_KEYS]
    52	
    53	
    54	def encode_rows(rows, id_of, nl):
    55	    """Token ids of ` {s} {e} {t}\\n` per row, and the span of each row. id_of maps a role to one token id."""
    56	    ids, spans = [], []
    57	    for s, e, t in rows:
    58	        spans.append({"s": s, "e": e, "t": t, "pos_s": len(ids), "pos_e": len(ids) + 1, "pos_t": len(ids) + 2})
    59	        ids.extend((id_of[s], id_of[e], id_of[t], nl))
    60	    return ids, spans
    61	
    62	
    63	def span_of(spans, key):
    64	    for sp in spans:
    65	        if (sp["s"], sp["e"]) == key:
    66	            return sp
    67	    raise KeyError(key)
    68	
    69	
    70	def p1_sequence(pi, filler, query, n):
    71	    """Table, then filler, then the two-token query. n is the full length. None if the table does not fit."""
    72	    gap = n - len(pi) - len(query)
    73	    if gap < 0:
    74	        return None
    75	    return list(pi) + [filler] * gap + list(query)
    76	
    77	
    78	def swap_sequence(pi, value_pos, s_id, e_id, t_old, filler, u, distance):
    79	    """u copies of (s, e, old destination) just before the query, filler before that.
    80	
    81	    The returned length puts the query event at `distance` tokens after `value_pos`.
    82	    None when u copies already exceed that distance.
    83	    """
    84	    if u < 1:
    85	        raise ValueError("swap history needs at least one use")
    86	    hist = []
    87	    for _ in range(u):
    88	        hist.extend((s_id, e_id, t_old))
    89	    hist.extend((s_id, e_id))
    90	    gap = distance - ((len(pi) + len(hist) - 1) - value_pos)
    91	    if gap < 0:
    92	        return None
    93	    return list(pi) + [filler] * gap + hist
    94	
    95	
    96	def trace(events, start="s0"):
    97	    s = start
    98	    out = []
    99	    for e in events:
   100	        nxt = DELTA[(s, e)]
   101	        out.append((s, e, nxt))
   102	        s = nxt
   103	    return out
   104	
   105	
   106	def events_for_delay(tau, reuses=REUSES):
   107	    """Events whose trace uses (q, star) for the first time at index tau, after the other rows have been reused.
   108	
   109	    tau is even and at least 16. `reuses` further (enter, star) pairs follow, so the held row is used again.
   110	    """
   111	    if tau < 16 or tau % 2:
   112	        raise ValueError(f"tau must be an even integer >= 16, got {tau}")
   113	    n_loop = tau - 10  # tau - 1 - len(PREAMBLE); even when tau is even
   114	    if n_loop < 0 or n_loop % 2:
   115	        raise ValueError(f"loop count {n_loop} for tau {tau}")
   116	    events = list(PREAMBLE) + ["loop"] * n_loop + ["enter", "star"]
   117	    events += ["enter", "star"] * reuses
   118	    if len(events) != tau + 1 + 2 * reuses:
   119	        raise ValueError("event length")
   120	    return events
   121	
   122	
   123	def held_uses(events):
   124	    return [i for i, (s, e, _t) in enumerate(trace(events)) if (s, e) == HELD]
   125	
   126	
   127	def _selftest():
   128	    assert DELTA[HELD] == HELD_DEST and SWAP_DEST != HELD_DEST
   129	    assert len(rows_state_major()) == 16
   130	    assert rows_state_major()[0][:2] == HELD
   131	    assert [r[:2] for r in swap_rows()] == list(SWAP_KEYS)
   132	    # preamble ends at s0 and has already reused the cycle rows and the enter row
   133	    pre = trace(PREAMBLE)
   134	    assert pre[-1][2] == "s0"
   135	    assert sum(1 for s, e, _t in pre if (s, e) == ("s0", "loop")) >= 2
   136	    assert sum(1 for s, e, _t in pre if (s, e) == ("s1", "loop")) >= 2
   137	    assert sum(1 for s, e, _t in pre if (s, e) == ("s0", "enter")) >= 2
   138	    assert HELD not in {(s, e) for s, e, _t in pre}
   139	    for tau in (16, 64, 512, 2048, 8192):
   140	        ev = events_for_delay(tau)
   141	        uses = held_uses(ev)
   142	        assert uses[0] == tau, (tau, uses[:4])
   143	        assert uses[1:] == [tau + 2 * (i + 1) for i in range(REUSES)]
   144	        walked = trace(ev)
   145	        # every held use really is the held row, and the destination is s2
   146	        assert all(walked[i][:2] == HELD and walked[i][2] == HELD_DEST for i in uses)
   147	        # cycle rows were reused before the late first use, and one of them was used recently
   148	        before = walked[:tau]
   149	        for key in (("s0", "loop"), ("s1", "loop"), ("s0", "enter")):
   150	            hits = [i for i, (s, e, _t) in enumerate(before) if (s, e) == key]
   151	            assert len(hits) >= 2, (tau, key, hits)
   152	        recent = {(s, e) for s, e, _t in before[-4:]}
   153	        assert ("s0", "loop") in recent or ("s1", "loop") in recent
   154	    ids = {r: i + 1 for i, r in enumerate(STATES + EVENTS)}
   155	    pi, spans = encode_rows(rows_state_major(), ids, nl=99)
   156	    assert len(pi) == 64 and spans[0]["pos_t"] == 2 and pi[2] == ids["s2"]
   157	    seq = p1_sequence(pi, 7, (ids["q"], ids["star"]), 256)
   158	    assert len(seq) == 256 and seq[-2:] == [ids["q"], ids["star"]]
   159	    assert p1_sequence(pi, 7, (1, 2), 10) is None
   160	    spi, ssp = encode_rows(swap_rows(), ids, nl=99)
     1	"""
     2	The pinned executor (DEMO_DESIGN §2.3 and §5.1, Theorem P): tables, the single-token name pool, packed training
     3	blocks, and the certified batch-1 path shared by certification and the run.
     4	
     5	    Π      the table rows alone, ` {s} {e} {t}\\n` per row, every name one token with one leading space (H1),
     6	           encoded in isolation at positions 0 … |Π|−1
     7	    tick   a two-token block [s][e] at positions |Π| and |Π|+1, seeing Π and itself; the next state is the argmax
     8	           over the whole vocabulary at the event position; the block is then discarded
     9	    train  one Π followed by several blocks, each at the same two positions, with a block-diagonal mask
    10	
    11	The row convention matches the PINNED branch (research/pinned/pin_tables.py), so its E−1/E0/E1 results apply.
    12	Torch is imported only inside the functions that need a model.
    13	"""
    14	
    15	from __future__ import annotations
    16	
    17	import random
    18	
    19	ROW_FMT = " {s} {e} {t}\n"
    20	POOL_SEED = 4321
    21	BLOCKED = set("""anal anus arse ass bastard bitch boob boobs butt cock cocks crap cum cunt damn dick dicks dildo fag
    22	fuck fucked fucker fucking gay homo jerk kill killer kills hitler nazi nigger nigga penis piss porn porno prick pussy
    23	rape raped rapist sex sexy shit slut sperm suck sucks tits twat vagina whore wank xxx""".split())
    24	
    25	
    26	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ names (H1)
    27	
    28	def _ids(tok, text):
    29	    return list(tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False).ids)
    30	
    31	
    32	def _candidates(tok):
    33	    """Single-token names with one leading space: lower-case words, capitalised words, upper-case codes of one to
    34	    three letters, single letters. Real families use all four kinds (left, Alice, LM, H)."""
    35	    out = []
    36	    for t, i in tok.get_vocab().items():
    37	        if not t.startswith("Ġ"):
    38	            continue
    39	        w = t[1:]
    40	        if not (w.isascii() and w.isalpha()):
    41	            continue
    42	        kind = ("lower" if w.islower() and 3 <= len(w) <= 9 else
    43	                "cap" if w[0].isupper() and w[1:].islower() and 3 <= len(w) <= 9 else
    44	                "code" if w.isupper() and 1 <= len(w) <= 3 else
    45	                "letter" if len(w) == 1 else None)
    46	        if kind and w.lower() not in BLOCKED and _ids(tok, " " + w) == [i]:
    47	            out.append((w, kind))
    48	    out.sort()
    49	    random.Random(POOL_SEED).shuffle(out)
    50	    return out
    51	
    52	
    53	def _fits(tok, w, wid, chosen, id_of, nl):
    54	    if _ids(tok, " " + w + "\n") != [wid, nl] or _ids(tok, "\n " + w) != [nl, wid]:
    55	        return False
    56	    for o in chosen:
    57	        oid = id_of[o]
    58	        if _ids(tok, f" {w} {o}") != [wid, oid] or _ids(tok, f" {o} {w}") != [oid, wid]:
    59	            return False
    60	    return True
    61	
    62	
    63	def name_pool(tok, n_train=240, n_test=120):
    64	    """Disjoint train and test names; every name stays one token beside every other name and beside a newline,
    65	    so a row's token ids are exactly (s, e, t, newline) and a block's are (s, e). Each split keeps the kinds'
    66	    proportions of the candidate list."""
    67	    nl = _ids(tok, "\n")
    68	    assert len(nl) == 1, "newline is not one token"
    69	    nl = nl[0]
    70	    cands = _candidates(tok)
    71	    cut = int(0.66 * len(cands))
    72	    id_of, pools = {}, {}
    73	    for split, src, n in (("train", cands[:cut], n_train), ("test", cands[cut:], n_test)):
    74	        chosen = []
    75	        for w, _kind in src:
    76	            if len(chosen) >= n:
    77	                break
    78	            wid = _ids(tok, " " + w)[0]
    79	            if w in id_of or wid in id_of.values():
    80	                continue
    81	            id_of[w] = wid
    82	            if _fits(tok, w, wid, [c for c in id_of if c != w], id_of, nl):
    83	                chosen.append(w)
    84	            else:
    85	                del id_of[w]
    86	        pools[split] = chosen
    87	    # Family alphabets the clock will walk by id: bare digits (Qwen splits digits, so " 1" is two tokens), single
    88	    # letters with a space, brackets. They are names by id only (no text round trip), and only in training.
    89	    for name, text in ([(d, d) for d in "0123456789"] + [(c, " " + c) for c in "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"]
    90	                       + [(b, b) for b in "()[]"]):
    91	        ids = _ids(tok, text)
    92	        if len(ids) == 1 and ids[0] not in id_of.values() and name not in id_of:
    93	            id_of[name] = ids[0]
    94	            pools["train"].append(name)
    95	    kinds = dict(cands)
    96	    return {"train": pools["train"], "test": pools["test"], "id_of": id_of, "nl": nl,
    97	            "kinds": {s: {k: sum(kinds.get(w) == k for w in pools[s]) for k in ("lower", "cap", "code", "letter")}
    98	                      | {"extra": sum(w not in kinds for w in pools[s])} for s in ("train", "test")}}
    99	
   100	
   101	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ tables
   102	
   103	def draw_table(rng, names, n=None, a=None, shape=None):
   104	    """A total table over n states and a events (n·a ≤ 256 rows). Shapes: random destinations, a permutation per
   105	    event (swaps, moves), a bounded counter (±1 or hold), mostly self-loops (filters, flags). Rows are written
   106	    state-major (as a person writes them) or shuffled, half each."""
   107	    n = n or rng.randint(2, 32)
   108	    a = a or rng.randint(2, 8)
   109	    shape = shape or rng.choices(["random", "perm", "counter", "sticky"], weights=[0.55, 0.2, 0.1, 0.15])[0]
   110	    picked = rng.sample(list(names), n + a)
   111	    states, events = picked[:n], picked[n:]
   112	    delta = {}
   113	    for j, e in enumerate(events):
   114	        if shape == "perm":
   115	            perm = states[:]
   116	            rng.shuffle(perm)
   117	            for s, t in zip(states, perm):
   118	                delta[(s, e)] = t
   119	        elif shape == "counter":
   120	            step = rng.choice([-1, 0, 1])
   121	            for i, s in enumerate(states):
   122	                delta[(s, e)] = states[min(n - 1, max(0, i + step))]
   123	        else:
   124	            for s in states:
   125	                stay = shape == "sticky" and rng.random() < 0.8
   126	                delta[(s, e)] = s if stay else rng.choice(states)
   127	    rows = [(s, e, delta[(s, e)]) for s in states for e in events]
   128	    if rng.random() < 0.5:
   129	        rng.shuffle(rows)
   130	    return {"states": states, "events": events, "rows": rows, "shape": shape, "start": states[0]}
   131	
   132	
   133	def table_text(table):
   134	    return "".join(ROW_FMT.format(s=s, e=e, t=t) for s, e, t in table["rows"])
   135	
   136	
   137	def encode_table(table, id_of, nl):
   138	    ids = []
   139	    for s, e, t in table["rows"]:
   140	        ids += [id_of[s], id_of[e], id_of[t], nl]
   141	    return ids

   144	def size_bin(n_rows):
   145	    return "4-16" if n_rows <= 16 else "17-64" if n_rows <= 64 else "65-128" if n_rows <= 128 else "129-256"

   147	
   148	def pinned_example(rng, pool, split="train", blocks=16):
   149	    """One packed training sequence: Π, then `blocks` (s, e) blocks, each labelled with δ(s, e)."""
   150	    table = draw_table(rng, pool[split])
   151	    pi = encode_table(table, pool["id_of"], pool["nl"])
   152	    pairs = [(s, e, t) for s, e, t in table["rows"]]
   153	    pick = rng.sample(pairs, min(blocks, len(pairs)))
   154	    while len(pick) < blocks:
   155	        pick.append(rng.choice(pairs))
   156	    io = pool["id_of"]
   157	    return {"pi": pi, "blocks": [(io[s], io[e]) for s, e, _ in pick], "labels": [io[t] for _, _, t in pick]}
   158	
   159	
   160	def packed(ex):
   161	    """Token ids, position ids, owner per position (-1 on Π, block index otherwise) and label positions."""
   162	    P = len(ex["pi"])
   163	    ids, pos, owner = list(ex["pi"]), list(range(P)), [-1] * P
   164	    lab_pos = []
   165	    for b, (s, e) in enumerate(ex["blocks"]):
   166	        ids += [s, e]
   167	        pos += [P, P + 1]
   168	        owner += [b, b]
   169	        lab_pos.append(len(ids) - 1)
   170	    return ids, pos, owner, lab_pos
   171	
   172	
   173	def packed_batch(exs, pad_id):
   174	    """Tensors for a micro-batch of packed examples: ids, positions, a boolean (B, 1, L, L) visibility mask
   175	    (True = may attend), and (row, column, label) triples for the loss."""
   176	    import torch
   177	    seqs = [packed(ex) for ex in exs]
   178	    L = max(len(s[0]) for s in seqs)
   179	    B = len(seqs)
   180	    ids = torch.full((B, L), pad_id, dtype=torch.long)
   181	    pos = torch.zeros((B, L), dtype=torch.long)
   182	    allow = torch.zeros((B, 1, L, L), dtype=torch.bool)
   183	    rows, cols, labels = [], [], []
   184	    for i, ((sid, spos, owner, lab_pos), ex) in enumerate(zip(seqs, exs)):
   185	        n = len(sid)
   186	        ids[i, :n] = torch.tensor(sid)
   187	        pos[i, :n] = torch.tensor(spos)
   188	        own = torch.tensor(owner)
   189	        P = int((own < 0).sum())
   190	        q = torch.arange(n)
   191	        causal = q[:, None] >= q[None, :]
   192	        same = own[:, None] == own[None, :]
   193	        to_pi = (own[None, :] < 0).expand(n, n)
   194	        allow[i, 0, :n, :n] = causal & (same | to_pi)
   195	        allow[i, 0, :P, P:n] = False
   196	        if n < L:
   197	            idx = torch.arange(n, L)
   198	            allow[i, 0, idx, idx] = True
   199	            pos[i, n:] = 0
   200	        for p, lab in zip(lab_pos, ex["labels"]):
   201	            rows.append(i)
   202	            cols.append(p)
   203	            labels.append(lab)
   204	    return ids, pos, allow, torch.tensor(rows), torch.tensor(cols), torch.tensor(labels)
   205	
   206	
   207	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ the certified path (batch 1, Cor. P2)
   208	
   209	def prefill(model, pi_ids):
   210	    import torch
   211	    ids = torch.tensor([pi_ids], device=model.device)
   212	    pos = torch.arange(len(pi_ids), device=model.device)[None]
   213	    with torch.no_grad():
   214	        return model(input_ids=ids, position_ids=pos, use_cache=True).past_key_values
   215	
   216	
   217	def _clone(past):
   218	    import copy
   219	    import torch
   220	    if hasattr(past, "layers"):
   221	        new = copy.copy(past)
   222	        new.layers = []
   223	        for layer in past.layers:
   224	            c = copy.copy(layer)
   225	            for attr in ("keys", "values"):
   226	                v = getattr(c, attr, None)
   227	                if torch.is_tensor(v):
   228	                    setattr(c, attr, v.clone())
   229	            new.layers.append(c)
   230	        return new
   231	    if hasattr(past, "key_cache"):
   232	        new = copy.copy(past)
   233	        new.key_cache = [t.clone() for t in past.key_cache]
   234	        new.value_cache = [t.clone() for t in past.value_cache]
   235	        return new
   236	    return tuple(tuple(t.clone() for t in layer) for layer in past)
   237	
   238	
   239	def tick(model, past, P, s_id, e_id):
   240	    """One tick: the block [s][e] at positions P, P+1 over a copy of Π's cache. Returns the full-vocabulary
   241	    logits at the event position (the copy is discarded, so Π's cache is never written)."""
   242	    import torch
   243	    pos = torch.tensor([[P, P + 1]], device=model.device)
   244	    with torch.no_grad():
   245	        out = model(input_ids=torch.tensor([[s_id, e_id]], device=model.device), position_ids=pos,
   246	                    cache_position=pos[0], past_key_values=_clone(past), use_cache=True)
   247	    return out.logits[0, -1]
   248	
   249	
   250	def top2(logits):
   251	    import torch
   252	    v, i = torch.topk(logits.float(), 2)
   253	    return int(i[0]), float(v[0] - v[1])
   254	
   255	
   256	def certify(model, pi_ids, rows, keep_logits=False):
   257	    """Every row of S × B, one batch-1 tick each over the same cached Π. Passes iff every argmax (full
   258	    vocabulary) is the row's destination id with a strictly positive margin."""
   259	    past = prefill(model, pi_ids)
   260	    P = len(pi_ids)
   280	                if line.lower().startswith(("model name", "vendor_id", "cpu family", "model\t"))]
   281	        flags = next((line.split(":", 1)[1].split() for line in info.splitlines() if line.startswith("flags")), [])
   282	        cpu = " | ".join(bits[:4]) + f" | avx512f={'avx512f' in flags}"
   283	    except Exception:
   284	        pass
   285	    node = hashlib.sha256(platform.node().encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
   286	    return {"gpu": gpu, "cpu": cpu, "host": node}

   289	def load_model(name, adapter):
   290	    import torch
   291	    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
   292	    spec = MODELS[name]
   293	    for key in ("HF_TOKEN", "HF_HUB_TOKEN", "HUGGING_FACE_HUB_TOKEN"):
   294	        os.environ.pop(key, None)
   295	    os.environ["HF_HUB_OFFLINE"] = "1"
   296	    os.environ["TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE"] = "1"
   297	    path = hub_snapshot(spec["repo"])
   298	    tok = Tok(AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(str(path), local_files_only=True))
   299	    kwargs = {"dtype": torch.bfloat16, "local_files_only": True}
   300	    if spec["family"] == "rope":
   301	        kwargs["attn_implementation"] = "sdpa"
   302	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(str(path), **kwargs)
   303	    merged = None
   304	    if spec["adapter"]:
   305	        from peft import PeftModel
   306	        weight = pathlib.Path(adapter) / "adapter_model.safetensors"
   307	        if not weight.is_file():
   308	            cands = sorted(pathlib.Path(adapter).glob("adapter_model.*"))
   309	            if not cands:
   310	                raise FileNotFoundError(f"no adapter weights in {adapter}")
   311	            weight = cands[0]
   312	        subprocess.run(["/bin/rm", "-rf", "/tmp/pr-merge"], check=False)
   313	        for stale in pathlib.Path("/tmp").glob("*merged*"):
   314	            subprocess.run(["/bin/rm", "-rf", str(stale)], check=False)
   315	        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, adapter).merge_and_unload()
   316	        merged = {"path": str(adapter), "sha256": file_sha256(weight), "merged": True, "saved": False}
   317	    model.eval()
   318	    for p in model.parameters():
   319	        p.requires_grad_(False)
   320	    model.cuda()
   321	    return model, tok, str(path), merged

   324	def load_pool(tok):
   325	    import pb_pin
   326	    pool = pb_pin.name_pool(tok)
   327	    if len(pool["test"]) >= 8:
   328	        pool["source"] = "pb_pin"
   329	        return pool
   330	    return _fallback_pool(tok)
   331	
   332	
   333	def _fallback_pool(tok):
   334	    """Used when the tokenizer has no Ġ word pool large enough for eight names."""
   335	    words = """the and for that with this from have they were been into than then them your what when
   336	        where which would there their about other could first after these those under over between
   337	        because before while three four five six seven eight nine alpha beta gamma delta north south
   338	        east west gold iron salt tree bird fish rock moon rain snow wind leaf seed root lake hill
   339	        path gate door room line mark note page left right front back name form type kind part side
   340	        step case fact idea plan test item unit area size rate cost time year week hour""".split()
   341	    nl_ids = list(tok.encode("\n", add_special_tokens=False).ids)
   342	    if len(nl_ids) != 1:
   343	        raise RuntimeError("newline is not one token")
   344	    nl = int(nl_ids[0])
   345	    id_of, chosen = {}, []
   346	    for word in words:
   347	        if len(chosen) >= 40:
   348	            break
   349	        ids = list(tok.encode(" " + word, add_special_tokens=False).ids)
   350	        if len(ids) != 1 or ids[0] in id_of.values() or word in id_of:
   351	            continue
   352	        wid = int(ids[0])
   353	        id_of[word] = wid
   354	        ok = list(tok.encode(" " + word + "\n", add_special_tokens=False).ids) == [wid, nl]
   355	        ok = ok and list(tok.encode("\n " + word, add_special_tokens=False).ids) == [nl, wid]
   356	        for other in chosen:
   357	            oid = id_of[other]
   358	            ok = ok and list(tok.encode(f" {word} {other}", add_special_tokens=False).ids) == [wid, oid]
   359	            ok = ok and list(tok.encode(f" {other} {word}", add_special_tokens=False).ids) == [oid, wid]
   360	            if not ok:
   361	                break
   362	        if ok:
   363	            chosen.append(word)
   364	        else:
   365	            del id_of[word]
   366	    if len(chosen) < 16:
   367	        raise RuntimeError(f"fallback name pool has {len(chosen)} names")
   368	    cut = len(chosen) // 2
   369	    return {"train": chosen[:cut], "test": chosen[cut:], "id_of": {w: id_of[w] for w in chosen},
   370	            "nl": nl, "source": "fallback"}
   371	
   372	
   373	def forward_last(model, ids):
   374	    import torch
   375	    x = torch.tensor([list(ids)], device=model.device)
   376	    with torch.no_grad():
   377	        out = model(input_ids=x, use_cache=False, logits_to_keep=1)
   378	    lg = out.logits[0, -1].float()
   379	    del out
   380	    return lg
   381	
   382	
   383	def decision(lg, target):
   384	    import torch
   385	    target = int(target)
   386	    pred = int(torch.argmax(lg))
   387	    top = float(lg[pred])
   388	    if pred == target:
   389	        masked = lg.clone()
   390	        masked[pred] = float("-inf")
   391	        margin = top - float(torch.max(masked))
   392	        del masked
   393	    else:
   394	        margin = float(lg[target]) - top
   395	    return pred, float(margin), int(pred == target)
   396	
   397	
   398	def linf(a, b):
   399	    import torch
   400	    return float(torch.max(torch.abs(a - b)))
   401	
   402	
   403	def pin_rows(model, pi, rows, id_of):
   404	    import pb_pin
   405	    past = pb_pin.prefill(model, pi)
   406	    out = []
   407	    for s, e, t in rows:
   408	        lg = pb_pin.tick(model, past, len(pi), id_of[s], id_of[e])
   409	        arg, margin = pb_pin.top2(lg)
   410	        out.append({"s": s, "e": e, "ok": int(arg == id_of[t] and margin > 0), "margin": float(margin),
   411	                    "query": f"{s}/{e}"})
   412	        del lg
   413	    return out

   494	
   495	def logits_at(model, ids, positions, chunk=64):
   496	    import torch
   497	    x = torch.tensor([list(ids)], device=model.device)
   498	    with torch.no_grad():
   499	        hidden = model.model(input_ids=x, use_cache=False).last_hidden_state[0]
   500	    cols = []
   501	    for i in range(0, len(positions), chunk):
   502	        sl = positions[i:i + chunk]
   503	        cols.append(model.lm_head(hidden[sl]).float())
   504	    out = torch.cat(cols, 0) if cols else hidden[:0]
   505	    del hidden, cols, x
   506	    return out

   508	
   509	class Pin:
   510	    def __init__(self, model, pi):
   511	        self.model = model
   512	        self.pi = list(pi)
   513	        self.past = None
   514	        self.cache = {}
   515	
   516	    def query(self, state, event, true):
   517	        key = (int(state), int(event))
   518	        if key in self.cache:
   519	            return self.cache[key]
   520	        import pb_pin
   521	        if self.past is None:
   522	            self.past = pb_pin.prefill(self.model, self.pi)
   523	        lg = pb_pin.tick(self.model, self.past, len(self.pi), int(state), int(event))
   524	        pred, margin, ok = pr_job.decision(lg.float(), int(true))
   525	        del lg
   526	        rec = (int(pred), round(float(margin), 4), int(ok))
   527	        self.cache[key] = rec
   528	        return rec

   531	def _pack(step, pos, state, event, pred, true, margin, pin):
   532	    return [int(step), int(pos), int(state), int(event), int(pred), None if true is None else int(true),
   533	            None if margin is None else round(float(margin), 4), None if pin is None else int(pin)]

   535	
   536	def _write_steps(out, name, rows):
   537	    blob = json.dumps(rows, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)
   538	    if len(blob.encode()) > 1_700_000:
   539	        raise RuntimeError(f"{name} is {len(blob)} bytes")
   540	    _atomic(pathlib.Path(out) / "steps" / name, blob)
   541	
   542	
   543	def _score_tf(model, ids, queries, pin, tau, out, name):
   544	    import torch
   545	    positions = [q["pos"] for q in queries]
   546	    try:
   547	        lgs = logits_at(model, ids, positions)
   548	    except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   549	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   550	        return None, "oom"
   551	    rows = []
   552	    n_err = pin_err = pin_n = 0
   553	    first = None
   554	    diverged = False
   555	    for i, q in enumerate(queries):
   556	        pred, margin, ok = pr_job.decision(lgs[i], q["true"])
   557	        pin_ok = None
   558	        if not ok:
   559	            n_err += 1
   560	            if q["step"] < tau:
   561	                diverged = True
   562	            _pred, _m, pok = pin.query(q["state"], q["event"], q["true"])
   563	            pin_ok = pok
   564	            pin_n += 1
   565	            pin_err += int(not pok)
   566	        if q.get("held") and first is None:
   567	            first = {"step": q["step"], "margin": round(float(margin), 4), "ok": int(ok), "pred": int(pred)}
   568	        rows.append(_pack(q["step"], q["pos"], q["state"], q["event"], pred, q["true"], margin, pin_ok))
   569	    del lgs
   570	    _write_steps(out, name, rows)
   571	    # Teacher forcing writes the true state, so the held row is fed even if an earlier step was wrong.
   572	    return {"first": first, "n_err": n_err, "n": len(rows), "pin_n": pin_n, "pin_err": pin_err,
   573	            "diverged_before_exposure": 0 if first is not None else 1,
   574	            "exposed": int(first is not None)}, None
   575	
   576	
   577	def _one(model, past, pos, tok_id):
   578	    import torch
   579	    pos_ids = torch.tensor([[pos]], device=model.device)
   580	    with torch.no_grad():
   581	        out_m = model(input_ids=torch.tensor([[int(tok_id)]], device=model.device), position_ids=pos_ids,
   582	                      cache_position=pos_ids[0], past_key_values=past, use_cache=True, logits_to_keep=1)
   583	    return out_m.past_key_values, out_m.logits[0, -1].float()
   584	
   585	
   586	def _free_run(model, past, pos0, queries, pin, tau, held_pair, dest_of, start_state, kind, out, name, t0, limit):
   587	    """Free-running decode. `trained` feeds the event and then the predicted state. `bare` feeds the state and
   588	    then the event; the predicted destination is the next state. An error before τ stops the arm: the held row
   589	    was not exposed."""
   590	    import torch
   591	    rows = []
   592	    n_err = pin_err = pin_n = 0
   593	    first = None
   594	    diverged = False
   595	    state_id = int(start_state)
   596	    pos = int(pos0)
   597	    try:
   598	        for q in queries:
   599	            if time.monotonic() - t0 > limit - 90:
   600	                _write_steps(out, name, rows)
   601	                return {"partial": 1, "n": len(rows), "diverged_before_exposure": 1, "exposed": 0}, "budget"
   602	            event = int(q["event"])
   603	            if kind == "bare":
   604	                past, _lg = _one(model, past, pos, state_id)
   605	                del _lg
   606	                pos += 1
   607	            past, lg = _one(model, past, pos, event)
   608	            true = dest_of.get((state_id, event))
   609	            held = state_id == int(held_pair[0]) and event == int(held_pair[1])
   610	            if true is None:
   611	                pred = int(torch.argmax(lg))
   612	                margin, ok, pin_ok = None, True, None
   613	                if q["step"] < tau:
   614	                    diverged = True
   615	            else:
   616	                pred, margin, ok = pr_job.decision(lg, true)
   617	                pin_ok = None
   618	                if not ok:
   619	                    n_err += 1
   620	                    _pred, _m, pok = pin.query(state_id, event, true)
   621	                    pin_ok = pok
   622	                    pin_n += 1
   623	                    pin_err += int(not pok)
   624	                    if q["step"] < tau:
   625	                        diverged = True
   626	            del lg
   627	            if held and first is None:
   628	                first = {"step": q["step"], "margin": None if margin is None else round(float(margin), 4),
   629	                         "ok": int(bool(ok) and true is not None and pred == true), "pred": int(pred)}
   630	            rows.append(_pack(q["step"], pos, state_id, event, pred, true, margin, pin_ok))
   631	            if kind == "trained":
   632	                pos += 1
   633	                past, _lg = _one(model, past, pos, pred)
   634	                del _lg
   635	                pos += 1
   636	            else:
   637	                pos += 1
   638	            state_id = int(pred)
   639	            if diverged and q["step"] < tau:
   640	                break
   641	            if q["step"] and q["step"] % 256 == 0:
   642	                print(json.dumps({"free": name, "step": q["step"], "pos": pos}), flush=True)
   643	    except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   644	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   645	        _write_steps(out, name, rows)
   646	        return {"partial": 1, "n": len(rows), "exposed": 0, "diverged_before_exposure": 1}, "oom"
   647	    _write_steps(out, name, rows)
   648	    exposed = first is not None and not (diverged and (first["step"] < tau))
   649	    # An error at the held step is exposure. An error before it is not.
   650	    if diverged and (first is None or first["step"] < tau):
   651	        exposed = False
   652	        first = None
   653	    return {"first": first, "n_err": n_err, "n": len(rows), "pin_n": pin_n, "pin_err": pin_err,
   654	            "diverged_before_exposure": int(not exposed), "exposed": int(exposed)}, None
   655	
   656	
   657	def _mark_held(queries, state_id, event_id, tau):
   658	    for q in queries:
   659	        q["held"] = int(q["state"] == state_id and q["event"] == event_id and q["step"] >= 0)
   660	    # the first held step is the exposure; later ones are reuses and stay marked
   661	    return queries
   662	
   663	
   664	def _overdue(t0, limit):
   665	    return time.monotonic() - t0 > limit - 90
   666	
   667	
   668	def run(args):
   669	    import torch
   670	    t0 = time.monotonic()
   671	    out = pathlib.Path(args.out)
   672	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   673	    prior = out / "runs.json"
   674	    state = json.loads(prior.read_text()) if prior.is_file() else {}
   675	    state.update({"schema": 2, "mode": "p2v2", "model": MODEL, "seed": int(args.seed), "unaudited": True,
   676	                  "complete": False})
   677	    print(json.dumps({"load": MODEL}), flush=True)
   678	    model, tok, snapshot, merged = pr_job.load_model(MODEL, args.adapter)
   679	    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
   680	    raw = Tokenizer.from_file(str(pathlib.Path(snapshot) / "tokenizer.json"))
   681	    otok = OffTok(raw)
   682	    state["snapshot"] = snapshot
   683	    state["adapter"] = merged
   684	    state["config"] = pr_job.config_record(model)
   685	    state["host"] = pr_job.host_record()
   686	    max_pos = int(state["config"]["max_position_embeddings"])
   687	    state["max_position_embeddings"] = max_pos
   688	    print(json.dumps({"config": {"max_position_embeddings": max_pos,
   689	                                 "sliding_window": state["config"].get("sliding_window")}}), flush=True)
   690	    pool = pr_job.load_pool(tok)
   691	    state.setdefault("cells", {})
   692	    state.setdefault("corruption", [])
   693	    state.setdefault("draws", [])
   694	    limit = int(args.max_seconds)
   695	
   696	    def persist():
   697	        save_state(out, state)
   698	
   699	    have = {d["k"]: d for d in state["draws"]}
   700	    passing = {"trained": [], "bare": []}
   701	    k = 0
   702	    while k < MAX_K and (len(passing["trained"]) < N_DRAWS or len(passing["bare"]) < N_DRAWS):
   703	        if _overdue(t0, limit):
   704	            state["budget_stop"] = "during gates"
   705	            break
   706	        rec = have.get(k)
   707	        if rec is None:
   708	            rec = {"k": k}
   709	            bare = pr_job._table_from_pool(pool, tok, k, int(args.seed))
   710	            rows = pr_data.rows_state_major()
   711	            pi, spans = pr_data.encode_rows(rows, bare["id_of"], bare["nl"])
   712	            rec["bare"] = {"names": bare["names"], "pi_len": len(pi)}
   713	            trained = draw_trained(otok, k, int(args.seed))
   714	            if "error" in trained:
   715	                rec["trained"] = {"error": trained["error"], "reasons": trained.get("reasons")}
   716	            else:
   717	                rec["trained"] = {"held": list(trained["held_names"]), "n_rows": trained["n_rows"],
   718	                                  "task": trained["task"], "fmt": trained["fmt"], "start": trained["start"]}
   719	            # gates
   720	            rec["gate"] = {}
   721	            for fmt in ("trained", "bare"):
   722	                rec["gate"][fmt] = _gate(model, fmt, rec, trained if "error" not in trained else None,
   723	                                         bare, rows, pi, otok, max_pos)
   724	            state["draws"].append(rec)
   725	            have[k] = rec
   726	            persist()
   727	            print(json.dumps({"gate": k, "trained": rec["gate"]["trained"].get("pass"),
   728	                              "bare": rec["gate"]["bare"].get("pass")}), flush=True)
   729	        for fmt in ("trained", "bare"):
   730	            if rec["gate"][fmt].get("pass") and k not in passing[fmt] and len(passing[fmt]) < N_DRAWS:
   731	                passing[fmt].append(k)
   732	        k += 1
   733	    state["passing"] = passing
   734	    state["dropped"] = [fmt for fmt in ("trained", "bare") if not passing[fmt]]
   735	    persist()
   736	
   737	    for fmt in ("trained", "bare"):
   738	        if fmt in state["dropped"]:
   739	            continue
   740	        for k in passing[fmt]:
   741	            if _overdue(t0, limit):
   742	                state["budget_stop"] = f"before {fmt} d{k}"
   743	                persist()
   744	                break
   745	            _run_draw(model, state, out, fmt, k, otok, tok, pool, max_pos, t0, limit, persist)
   746	        if state.get("budget_stop"):
   747	            break
   748	    state["complete"] = not bool(state.get("budget_stop"))
   749	    state["seconds"] = round(time.monotonic() - t0, 3)
   750	    persist()
   751	    print(json.dumps({"complete": state["complete"], "budget": state.get("budget_stop"),
   752	                      "seconds": state["seconds"]}), flush=True)
   753	
   754	
   755	def _gate(model, fmt, rec, trained, bare, rows, pi, otok, max_pos):
   756	    import torch
   757	    if fmt == "trained":
   758	        if trained is None:
   759	            return {"pass": 0, "why": "no program"}
   760	        cover = covering_or_none(trained["transit"], trained["states"], trained["events"], trained["start"], GATE_LEN)
   761	        if cover is None:
   762	            ev = first_use_events(trained["transit"], trained["states"], trained["events"],
   763	                                  trained["start"], GATE_LEN, trained["held"])
   764	            kind = "first-use"
   765	        else:
   766	            ev = cover
   767	            kind = "covering"
   768	        if ev is None:
   769	            return {"pass": 0, "why": "no gate tape"}
   770	        made, why = trained["make"](ev)
   771	        if made is None:
   772	            return {"pass": 0, "why": why}
   773	        lay = made["lay"]
   774	        if lay["n"] > max_pos:
   775	            return {"pass": 0, "why": "gate exceeds context"}
   776	        reg = made["lit"]["registers"][0]
   777	        id_of = {n: pb_lit.name_id(otok, n) for n in list(reg["name_of"].values()) + made["lit"]["events"]}
   778	        pin_rows = [(s, e, t) for s, e, t in reg["rows"]]
   779	        program = lay["ids"][lay["prog_start"]:lay["prog_start"] + lay["prog_len"]]
   780	        try:
   781	            pins = pr_job.pin_rows(model, program, pin_rows, id_of)
   782	        except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   783	            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   784	            return {"pass": 0, "why": "oom"}
   785	        start_id = pb_lit.name_id(otok, reg["start"])
   786	        queries = _queries_trained(lay, start_id)
   787	        try:
   788	            lgs = logits_at(model, lay["ids"][:queries[-1]["pos"] + 1], [q["pos"] for q in queries])
   789	        except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   790	            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   791	            return {"pass": 0, "why": "oom"}
   792	        wrong = 0
   793	        for i, q in enumerate(queries):
   794	            _pred, margin, ok = pr_job.decision(lgs[i], q["true"])
   795	            if not ok or margin <= 0:
   796	                wrong += 1
   797	        del lgs
   798	        pin_wrong = sum(not r["ok"] for r in pins)
   799	        covered = kind == "covering"
   800	        # The pin reads every row. A covering run also queries every row inside the delay-16 tape.
   801	        return {"pass": int(pin_wrong == 0 and wrong == 0), "why": kind, "pin_wrong": pin_wrong,
   802	                "run_wrong": wrong, "n": len(queries), "covered_in_16": int(covered), "tokens": lay["n"]}
   803	    # bare
   804	    cover = covering_or_none(lambda s, e: pr_data.DELTA[(s, e)], pr_data.STATES, pr_data.EVENTS, "s0", GATE_LEN)
   805	    try:
   806	        pins = pr_job.pin_rows(model, pi, rows, bare["id_of"])
   807	    except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   808	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   809	        return {"pass": 0, "why": "oom"}
   810	    ev = pr_data.events_for_delay(GATE_LEN)
   811	    queries = _queries_bare(len(pi), ev, bare["id_of"], lambda s, e: pr_data.DELTA[(s, e)], "s0")
   812	    ids = bare_ids(pi, ev, bare["id_of"], lambda s, e: pr_data.DELTA[(s, e)], "s0")
   813	    try:
   814	        lgs = logits_at(model, ids, [q["pos"] for q in queries])
   815	    except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
   816	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   817	        return {"pass": 0, "why": "oom"}
   818	    wrong = 0
   819	    for i, q in enumerate(queries):
   820	        _pred, margin, ok = pr_job.decision(lgs[i], q["true"])
   821	        if not ok or margin <= 0:
   822	            wrong += 1
   823	    del lgs
   824	    pin_wrong = sum(not r["ok"] for r in pins)
   825	    return {"pass": int(pin_wrong == 0 and wrong == 0), "why": "pin+delay16" if cover is None else "covering",
   826	            "pin_wrong": pin_wrong, "run_wrong": wrong, "n": len(queries),
   827	            "covered_in_16": 0 if cover is None else 1, "tokens": len(ids)}
   828	
   829	
   830	def _run_draw(model, state, out, fmt, k, otok, tok, pool, max_pos, t0, limit, persist):
   831	    rec = next(d for d in state["draws"] if d["k"] == k)

   838	        held = pr_data.HELD
   839	        id_of = bare["id_of"]
   840	        pin = Pin(model, pi)

   912	    def one_cell(arm, tau):
   913	        key = f"{fmt}|{k}|{arm}|{tau}"
   914	        if key in state["cells"]:
   915	            return False
   916	        if _overdue(t0, limit):
   917	            state["budget_stop"] = key

   919	            return True

   924	            queries, made = queries_of(ev)

   928	            queries = queries_of(ev)

   933	        if arm == "tf":
   934	            # The logit is read at the event. Tokens after that event are not required.
   935	            fed = ids[:queries[-1]["pos"] + 1]

   937	        else:

   939	            past = _pb.prefill(model, ids[:run_start])

   941	                                    dest_of, start_state, kind, out, name, t0, limit)

   943	        if scored:

   949	    def corrupt():
   950	        # Short distances first, then the largest gap that fits. Returns True when the budget is spent.
   951	        targets = list(CORRUPT_TARGETS)
   952	        g_max = _largest_gap(fmt, max_pos, transit, states, events_abc, start, held, stride,
   953	                             None if fmt == "bare" else trained, len(pi) if fmt == "bare" else None)
   954	        if g_max is not None and g_max >= 2:
   955	            dist = token_distance(g_max, stride)
   956	            if dist not in [token_distance(gap_for(t, stride), stride) for t in targets]:
   957	                targets.append(dist)
   958	        for target in targets:
   959	            if _overdue(t0, limit):
   960	                state["budget_stop"] = f"{fmt} d{k} corruption {target}"
   961	                persist()
   962	                return True
   963	            g = gap_for(target, stride) if target in CORRUPT_TARGETS else g_max
   964	            key = f"{fmt}|{k}|corr|{g}"
   965	            if any(c.get("key") == key for c in state["corruption"]):
   966	                continue
   967	            built = corruption_events(transit, states, events_abc, start, held, g)
   968	            if built is None:
   969	                state["corruption"].append({"key": key, "error": "no tape", "target": target})
   970	                persist()
   971	                continue
   972	            ev, first = built
   973	            if fmt == "trained":
   974	                made, why = trained["make"](ev)
   975	                if made is None or made["lay"]["n"] > max_pos:
   976	                    state["corruption"].append({"key": key, "error": why or "context", "target": target})
   977	                    persist()
   978	                    continue
   979	                queries = _queries_trained(made["lay"], start_id)
   980	                ids = made["lay"]["ids"]
   981	                out_pos = made["lay"]["run_start"] + first * stride + 1
   982	            else:
   983	                queries = queries_of(ev)
   984	                ids = ids_of(ev)
   985	                # The bare destination is written as the next step's state token.
   986	                out_pos = queries[first + 1]["state_pos"]
   987	            reuse = queries[first + g]
   988	            if reuse["state_pos"] == out_pos:
   989	                state["corruption"].append({"key": key, "error": "overwrite collides with the reuse", "target": target})
   990	                persist()
   991	                continue
   992	            true_out = ids[out_pos]
   993	            if fmt == "trained":
   994	                cands = [i for i in made["lay"]["states"][0] if i != true_out]
   995	            else:
   996	                cands = [id_of[s] for s in pr_data.STATES if id_of[s] != true_out]
   997	            wrong = cands[0]
   998	            dirty = list(ids)
   999	            dirty[out_pos] = wrong
  1000	            fed_c = ids[:reuse["pos"] + 1]
  1001	            fed_d = dirty[:reuse["pos"] + 1]
  1002	            try:
  1003	                lg_c = logits_at(model, fed_c, [reuse["pos"]])[0]
  1004	                lg_d = logits_at(model, fed_d, [reuse["pos"]])[0]
  1005	            except torch.OutOfMemoryError:
  1006	                import torch
  1007	                torch.cuda.empty_cache()
  1008	                state["corruption"].append({"key": key, "error": "oom", "target": target, "g": g})
  1009	                persist()
  1010	                continue
  1011	            _pc, mc, oc = pr_job.decision(lg_c, reuse["true"])
  1012	            pd, md, _od = pr_job.decision(lg_d, reuse["true"])
  1013	            del lg_c, lg_d
  1014	            follow_t = int(pd == reuse["true"])
  1015	            follow_h = int(pd == wrong)
  1016	            state["corruption"].append({
  1017	                "key": key, "draw": k, "format": fmt, "target": int(target), "g": int(g),
  1018	                "distance": int(reuse["pos"] - out_pos), "first": int(first),
  1019	                "control_ok": int(oc), "control_margin": round(float(mc), 4),
  1020	                "follow_t": follow_t, "follow_h": follow_h,
  1021	                "neither": int(follow_t == 0 and follow_h == 0),
  1022	                "corrupt_margin": round(float(md), 4),
  1023	            })
  1024	            persist()
  1025	            print(json.dumps({"corruption": key, "distance": int(reuse["pos"] - out_pos),
  1026	                              "follow_h": follow_h, "follow_t": follow_t, "control_ok": int(oc)}), flush=True)
  1027	        return False
  1028	
  1029	    # Teacher-forced exposure first, then corruption, then free-runs. A budget stop keeps the earlier cells.
  1030	    for tau in taus:
  1031	        if one_cell("tf", tau):
  1032	            return
  1033	    if corrupt():
  1034	        return
  1035	    for tau in taus:
  1036	        if one_cell("free", tau):
  1037	            return

  1040	def _largest_gap(fmt, max_pos, transit, states, events, start, held, stride, trained, pi_len):
  1041	    if fmt == "bare":
  1042	        probe = corruption_events(transit, states, events, start, held, 2)
  1043	        if probe is None:
  1044	            return None
  1045	        _ev, first = probe
  1046	        hi = (max_pos - pi_len) // stride - first - 1
  1047	        lo, best = 2, None
  1048	        while lo <= hi:
  1049	            mid = (lo + hi) // 2
  1050	            ev = corruption_events(transit, states, events, start, held, mid)
  1051	            if ev is not None and pi_len + stride * (ev[1] + mid + 1) <= max_pos:
  1052	                best = mid
  1053	                lo = mid + 1
  1054	            else:
  1055	                hi = mid - 1
  1056	        return best
  1057	    lo, hi = 2, max_pos
  1058	    best = None
  1059	    while lo <= hi:
  1060	        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
  1061	        ev = corruption_events(transit, states, events, start, held, mid)
  1062	        if ev is None:
  1063	            hi = mid - 1
  1064	            continue
  1065	        made, _why = trained["make"](ev[0])
  1066	        if made is not None and made["lay"]["n"] <= max_pos:
  1067	            best = mid
  1068	            lo = mid + 1
  1069	        else:
  1070	            hi = mid - 1
  1071	    return best

  1074	def _median(xs):
  1075	    xs = sorted(xs)
  1076	    n = len(xs)
  1077	    if n == 0:
  1078	        return None
  1079	    if n % 2:
  1080	        return xs[n // 2]
  1081	    return (xs[n // 2 - 1] + xs[n // 2]) / 2
  1082

  1084	def report_md(path):
  1085	    state = json.loads((pathlib.Path(path) / "runs.json").read_text())
  1086	    lines = ["## P2-v2 results", "", "Unaudited. An independent audit follows before anyone believes them.", ""]
  1087	    host = state.get("host") or {}
