  1089	                 f"gpu {host.get('gpu')}.")
  1090	    lines.append(f"CPU {host.get('cpu')}.")
  1091	    cfg = state.get("config") or {}
  1092	    lines.append(f"max_position_embeddings {state.get('max_position_embeddings')}. "
  1093	                 f"Sliding window {cfg.get('sliding_window')}.")
  1094	    adapter = state.get("adapter") or {}
  1095	    if adapter.get("sha256"):
  1096	        lines.append(f"Adapter sha256 {adapter['sha256']}.")
  1097	    lines.append(f"Complete {state.get('complete')}. Budget stop {state.get('budget_stop')}. "
  1098	                 f"Seconds {state.get('seconds')}.")
  1099	    lines.append(f"Dropped formats {state.get('dropped')}. Passing draws {state.get('passing')}.")
  1100	    lines.append("")
  1101	    for draw in state.get("draws") or []:
  1102	        lines.append(f"- draw {draw['k']}: gate {json.dumps(draw.get('gate'), sort_keys=True)}")
  1103	    lines.append("")
  1104	    lines.append("First-use margin by format, arm, draw and τ. A missing free-run margin diverged before exposure.")
  1105	    cells = state.get("cells") or {}
  1106	    for key in sorted(cells):
  1107	        cell = cells[key]
  1108	        first = cell.get("first")
  1109	        lines.append(f"- {key}: exposed {cell.get('exposed')} margin {None if not first else first.get('margin')} "
  1110	                     f"ok {None if not first else first.get('ok')} errors {cell.get('n_err')} of {cell.get('n')} "
  1111	                     f"pin errors {cell.get('pin_err')} of {cell.get('pin_n')} "
  1112	                     f"diverged {cell.get('diverged_before_exposure')} error {cell.get('error')} "
  1113	                     f"seconds {cell.get('seconds')}.")
  1114	    lines.append("")
  1115	    for fmt in ("trained", "bare"):
  1116	        if fmt in (state.get("dropped") or []):
  1117	            lines.append(f"{fmt}: out of distribution at delay 16, dropped from the long-delay analysis.")
  1118	            continue
  1119	        points = []
  1120	        taus = []
  1121	        info = (state.get("tau") or {}).get(fmt) or {}
  1122	        for k, rec in info.items():
  1123	            taus = rec.get("ladder") or taus
  1124	        for tau in taus:
  1125	            margins = []
  1126	            for k in (state.get("passing") or {}).get(fmt) or []:
  1127	                cell = cells.get(f"{fmt}|{k}|tf|{tau}") or {}
  1128	                first = cell.get("first")
  1129	                if first and first.get("margin") is not None and first.get("step") == tau:
  1130	                    margins.append(first["margin"])
  1131	            if margins:
  1132	                points.append((tau, _median(margins), len(margins)))
  1133	        lines.append(f"{fmt} teacher-forced median first-use margin: {points}.")
  1134	        if len(points) < 2:
  1135	            lines.append(f"{fmt} fall: untested.")
  1136	        elif any(b[1] >= a[1] for a, b in zip(points, points[1:])):
  1137	            lines.append(f"{fmt} fall: flat or rising. Falsifier fires on the trained format." if fmt == "trained"
  1138	                         else f"{fmt} fall: flat or rising.")
  1139	        else:
  1140	            lines.append(f"{fmt} fall: each longer τ has a smaller median margin.")
  1141	    lines.append("")
  1142	    corr = state.get("corruption") or []
  1143	    lines.append("Corruption. follow_h copies the overwritten destination; follow_t follows the table; "
  1144	                 "control_ok is the uncorrupted twin.")
  1145	    for row in corr:
  1146	        lines.append(f"- {json.dumps(row, sort_keys=True)}")
  1147	    by = {}
  1148	    for row in corr:
  1149	        if row.get("follow_h") is None:
  1150	            continue
  1151	        by.setdefault((row["format"], row["distance"]), []).append(row)
  1152	    for key, rows in sorted(by.items(), key=lambda kv: (kv[0][0], kv[0][1])):
  1153	        n = len(rows)
  1154	        copies = sum(r["follow_h"] == 1 for r in rows)
  1155	        errs = sum(r["control_ok"] == 0 for r in rows)
  1156	        lines.append(f"{key[0]} distance {key[1]}: copies {copies} of {n}, uncorrupted errors {errs} of {n}.")
  1157	    if not any(r.get("follow_h") == 1 for r in corr):
  1158	        lines.append("Corrupted history was not copied at any recorded distance.")
  1159	    else:
  1160	        lines.append("Corrupted history was copied at least once.")

    59	def _tape_positions(tok, prompt, question, lit):
    60	    """Token indices (in the encoded prompt) of the tape's events, by the clock's own walk (pb_lit.walk)."""
    61	    q0 = prompt.find(question)
    62	    assert q0 >= 0
    63	    a, b = pb_lit.find_span(question, *lit["anchors"])
    64	    a, b = a + q0, b + q0
    65	    e = tok.encode(prompt, add_special_tokens=False)
    66	    names = set(lit["events"])
    67	    out = []
    68	    for i, (s, t) in enumerate(e.offsets):
    69	        raw = prompt[s:t]
    70	        s2, t2 = s + len(raw) - len(raw.lstrip()), t - (len(raw) - len(raw.rstrip()))
    71	        if t2 <= a or s2 >= b:
    72	            continue
    73	        if prompt[s2:t2] in names:
    74	            out.append(i)
    75	    return out
    76	
    77	
    78	def layout(tok, ex, lit):
    79	    """ids of the whole plain-mode sequence and the index of every part. Run step k (0-based) has its event at
    80	    run_start + k*stride and register r's value at run_start + k*stride + 1 + r."""
    81	    prompt = enc(tok, ex["prompt"])
    82	    prog = pb_lit.program_ids(tok, lit)
    83	    # Π positions, rebuilt piece by piece exactly as pb_lit.program_ids builds the ids
    84	    nl = enc(tok, "\n")
    85	    import json as _json
    86	    head = enc(tok, f"<program>\ntask: {lit['task']}\ninput: {lit['input']}\n")
    87	    before, after = lit["anchors"]
    88	    head += enc(tok, "tape: after " + _json.dumps(before, ensure_ascii=False) + " until "
    89	                + _json.dumps(after, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    90	    pos = len(prompt) + len(head)
    91	    rows, rebuilt = [], list(head)
    92	    for r in lit["registers"]:
    93	        reg_head = enc(tok, f"register {r['name']}: start") + [pb_lit.name_id(tok, r["start"])] + nl
    94	        rebuilt += reg_head
    95	        pos += len(reg_head)
    96	        rr = []
    97	        for s, e, t in r["rows"]:
    98	            ids = [pb_lit.name_id(tok, s), pb_lit.name_id(tok, e), pb_lit.name_id(tok, t)]
    99	            rr.append({"pos": pos, "s": ids[0], "e": ids[1], "t": ids[2]})
   100	            rebuilt += ids + nl
   101	            pos += 4
   102	        rows.append(rr)
   103	    rebuilt += enc(tok, f"answer: {lit['answer_line']}\n</program>\n<exec/>")
   104	    assert rebuilt == prog, "program layout does not match pb_lit.program_ids"
   105	    opener = enc(tok, "\n<run>\n")
   106	    run = pb_lit.run_ids(tok, lit)
   107	    closer = enc(tok, "\n</run>\n")
   108	    ids = prompt + prog + opener + run + closer
   109	    R = len(lit["registers"])
   110	    tape_pos = _tape_positions(tok, ex["prompt"], ex["question"], lit)
   111	    reg_states = [sorted({pb_lit.name_id(tok, v) for v in r["name_of"].values()}) for r in lit["registers"]]
   112	    events = sorted({pb_lit.name_id(tok, e) for e in lit["events"]})
   113	    table = [{(x["s"], x["e"]): x["t"] for x in rr} for rr in rows]
   114	    starts = [pb_lit.name_id(tok, r["start"]) for r in lit["registers"]]
   115	    return {"ids": ids, "prompt_len": len(prompt), "prog_start": len(prompt), "prog_len": len(prog),
   116	            "run_start": len(prompt) + len(prog) + len(opener), "T": len(lit["tape"]), "R": R, "stride": 1 + R,
   117	            "rows": rows, "tape_pos": tape_pos, "states": reg_states, "events": events, "table": table,
   118	            "starts": starts, "n": len(ids)}
   119	
   120	
   121	def run_tokens(lay):
   122	    """(index, kind, step, register) for every run token; kind 'e' (read) or 's' (step)."""
   123	    out = []
   124	    for k in range(lay["T"]):
   125	        p = lay["run_start"] + k * lay["stride"]
   126	        out.append((p, "e", k, -1))
   127	        for r in range(lay["R"]):
   128	            out.append((p + 1 + r, "s", k, r))
   129	    return out
   130	
   131	
   132	def examples(tok, families, lengths, n, seed, split="train", max_tokens=CONTEXT - 64):
   133	    """n examples per (family, length), drawn as pb_loop draws its gate cells; skipped if unpinnable or over context.
   134	    Each cell has its own generator, seeded by (seed, family, length), so a cell's examples do not depend on which
   135	    other cells a job draws."""
   136	    out, skipped = [], {}
   137	    for fam in families:
   138	        for L in lengths:
   139	            rng = random.Random(f"{seed}|{fam}|{L}")
   140	            made, tries = 0, 0
   141	            while made < n and tries < 20 * n:
   142	                tries += 1
   143	                ex = family_example(rng, fam, L=L, split=split)
   144	                lit, why = pb_lit.literal(ex, tok)
   145	                if lit is None:
   146	                    skipped[f"{fam}|L{L}"] = skipped.get(f"{fam}|L{L}", 0) + 1
   147	                    continue
   148	                lay = layout(tok, ex, lit)
   149	                if lay["n"] > max_tokens:
   150	                    skipped[f"{fam}|L{L}|over_context"] = skipped.get(f"{fam}|L{L}|over_context", 0) + 1
   151	                    continue
   152	                assert len(lay["tape_pos"]) == lay["T"], (fam, L, len(lay["tape_pos"]), lay["T"])
   153	                out.append({"family": fam, "L": L, "cell": f"{fam}|L{L}", "ex": ex, "lit": lit, "lay": lay})
   154	                made += 1
   155	    return out, skipped
   156

   158	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ model
   159	
   160	def load_model(adapter=SAVANT, model=MODEL):
     1	"""
     2	Literal-table programs and span-quoted tapes for the pinned mode (DEMO_DESIGN §2.1, §2.4 R2, §2.6).
     3	
     4	A family question becomes this response (ordinary decoding):
     5	
     6	    <program>
     7	    task: which spot hides the coin after the swaps
     8	    input: the swaps in order
     9	    tape: after "Swaps, in order: " until ". Where is the coin"
    10	    register ball: start C
    11	     A AB B
    12	     A BC A
    13	     …                                   one row per (state, event): ` s e t` and a newline, each name one token
    14	    answer: The coin is under {ball}.
    15	    </program>
    16	    <exec/>
    17	
    18	Then the clock (pb_loop) takes over:
    19	- tape: the prompt's own tokens between the quoted prose (after the first quote, up to the second). Each token's
    20	  text, stripped, is either an event name
    21	  (the table's own id for that name goes on the tape) or a separator (empty, comma, semicolon, full stop);
    22	  anything else rejects the tape.
    23	- run: per register, Π = that register's row tokens exactly as written; one pinned tick per event (pb_pin).
    24	- it appends `<final> ball=B </final>`, and the model writes the answer in words.
    25	
    26	Plain decoding, the comparison mode: after `<exec/>` the harness appends `<run>` and the model writes the run
    27	itself, one event then each register's value per step, then `</run>` and the answer.
    28	
    29	Only literal rows: every register must be independent (it reads no other register), every value and event must
    30	have a one-token name, and the rows are what the program's rules give, written out. Families that need
    31	arithmetic over large ranges (counts, sums, remainders) are not pinnable here and keep the plain format.
    32	"""
    33	
    34	from __future__ import annotations
    35	
    36	import json
    37	import re
    38	
    39	from pb_lang import ProgramError, deps, parse_program, run_program, step_reg
    40	
    41	NUM_WORDS = ("zero one two three four five six seven eight nine ten eleven twelve thirteen fourteen fifteen sixteen "
    42	             "seventeen eighteen nineteen twenty").split()
    43	SEPARATORS = {"", ",", ";", "."}
    44	MAX_ROWS = 256
    45	
    46	
    47	def _one(tok, text):
    48	    ids = tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False).ids
    49	    return ids[0] if len(ids) == 1 else None
    50	
    51	
    52	def name_id(tok, name):
    53	    """The one-token id of a name: with a leading space if that is one token, else bare (digits, brackets)."""
    54	    return _one(tok, " " + name) or _one(tok, name)
    55	
    56	
    57	def _value_names(tok, reg):
    58	    """Numeric registers: bare digits when every value is at most 9, number words up to twenty otherwise.
    59	    Other registers: their own names, if each is one token."""
    60	    disp = [str(reg.disp(v)) for v in reg.values]
    61	    if all(d.isdigit() for d in disp):
    62	        nums = [int(d) for d in disp]
    63	        if max(nums) <= 9:
    64	            return [str(n) for n in nums]
    65	        if max(nums) < len(NUM_WORDS):
    66	            return [NUM_WORDS[n] for n in nums]
    67	        return None
    68	    if all(name_id(tok, d) for d in disp) and len({name_id(tok, d) for d in disp}) == len(disp):
    69	        return disp
    70	    return None
    71	
    72	
    73	def item_span(question, items):
    74	    """Character span of the items in the question: the first place where they occur in order with only
    75	    separators (spaces, commas, semicolons, newlines) between them. None if they do not."""
    76	    first = str(items[0])
    77	    for m in re.finditer(re.escape(first), question):
    78	        pos, ok, starts = m.end(), True, [m.start()]
    79	        for it in items[1:]:
    80	            it = str(it)
    81	            k = pos
    82	            while k < len(question) and question[k] in " ,;\n\t":
    83	                k += 1
    84	            if not question.startswith(it, k):
    85	                ok = False
    86	                break
    87	            starts.append(k)
    88	            pos = k + len(it)
    89	        if ok:
    90	            return m.start(), pos, starts
    91	    return None
    92	
    93	
    94	def anchors(question, items, min_words=3, max_chars=80):
    95	    """The prose just before the first item and just after the last, quoted exactly. Each quote starts at min_words
    96	    words and grows a word at a time: `before` until it occurs once in the question, `after` until its first
    97	    occurrence after the list's start is the list's end. An empty quote means the start or end of the question.
    98	    Item anchors (the first and last few items) were dropped after the Stage 2 pilot: lists repeat, and a miscopied
    99	    tail usually occurs earlier in the list."""
   100	    span = item_span(question, items)
   101	    if span is None:
   102	        return None
   103	    a, b, _ = span
   104	    pre, post = question[:a], question[b:]
   105	    before = "" if not pre.strip() else None
   106	    words = list(re.finditer(r"\S+\s*", pre))
   107	    for w in range(min(min_words, len(words)), len(words) + 1):
   108	        if before is not None:
   109	            break
   110	        cand = pre[words[-w].start():]
   111	        if len(cand) > max_chars:
   112	            return None
   113	        if question.count(cand) == 1:
   114	            before = cand
   115	    after = "" if not post.strip() else None
   116	    words = list(re.finditer(r"\s*\S+", post))
   117	    for w in range(min(min_words, len(words)), len(words) + 1):
   118	        if after is not None:
   119	            break
   120	        cand = post[:words[w - 1].end()]
   121	        if len(cand) > max_chars:
   122	            return None
   123	        if question.find(cand, a) == b:
   124	            after = cand
   125	    if before is None or after is None:
   126	        return None
   127	    return before, after
   128	
   129	
   130	def find_span(question, before, after):
   131	    """The clock's reading of a tape line: from the end of the first occurrence of `before` (or the question's start)
   132	    to the first occurrence of `after` after that (or the question's end)."""
   133	    start = 0
   134	    if before:
   135	        i = question.find(before)
   136	        if i < 0:
   137	            return None
   138	        start = i + len(before)
   139	    end = len(question)
   140	    if after:
   141	        j = question.find(after, start)
   142	        if j < 0:
   143	            return None
   144	        end = j
   145	    return start, end
   146	
   147	
   148	def walk(tok, question, span, event_names):
   149	    """Events in the span, in order, by the text of each prompt token (stripped). Returns (events, None) or
   150	    (None, reason)."""
   151	    enc = tok.encode(question, add_special_tokens=False)
   152	    a, b = span
   153	    names = set(event_names)
   154	    out = []
   155	    for (s, e) in enc.offsets:
   156	        raw = question[s:e]
   157	        s, e = s + len(raw) - len(raw.lstrip()), e - (len(raw) - len(raw.rstrip()))
   158	        if e <= a or s >= b:
   159	            continue
   160	        if s < a or e > b:
   161	            return None, f"token crosses the span edge at {s}"
   162	        text = question[s:e]
   163	        if text in names:
   164	            out.append(text)
   165	        elif text not in SEPARATORS:
   166	            return None, f"not an event or separator: {text!r}"
   167	    return out, None
   168	
   169	
   170	def literal(ex, tok):
   171	    """The literal-table program for a family example, or None with a reason."""
   172	    try:
   173	        prog = parse_program(ex["prog_text"])
   174	    except ProgramError as exc:
   175	        return None, f"program: {exc}"
   176	    if prog.other:
   177	        return None, "has an 'other' event class"
   178	    for r in prog.registers:
   179	        if deps(prog, r) not in ([r.name], []):
   180	            return None, f"register {r.name} reads another register"
   181	    events = prog.event_classes()
   182	    if not all(name_id(tok, e) for e in events) or len({name_id(tok, e) for e in events}) != len(events):
   183	        return None, "an event has no one-token name"
   184	    regs = []
   185	    for r in prog.registers:
   186	        names = _value_names(tok, r)
   187	        if names is None:
   188	            return None, f"register {r.name} has values without one-token names"
   189	        if len(r.values) * len(events) > MAX_ROWS:
   190	            return None, f"register {r.name} has {len(r.values) * len(events)} rows"
   191	        name_of = dict(zip(r.values, names))
   192	        rows = []
   193	        for v in r.values:
   194	            for e in events:
   195	                state = dict(prog.start)
   196	                state[r.name] = v
   197	                rows.append((name_of[v], e, name_of[step_reg(prog, r, state, e)]))
   198	        regs.append({"name": r.name, "start": name_of[prog.start[r.name]], "rows": rows, "name_of": name_of})
   199	    items = [prog.classify(it) for it in ex["items"]]
   200	    anc = anchors(ex["question"], ex["items"])
   201	    if anc is None:
   202	        return None, "items are not a separator-delimited list in the question"
   203	    span = find_span(ex["question"], *anc)
   204	    tape, why = walk(tok, ex["question"], span, events) if span else (None, "anchors not found")
   205	    if tape != [str(i) for i in items]:
   206	        return None, f"the clock would misread the tape ({why})"
   207	    steps, final = run_program(prog, ex["items"])
   208	    task = re.search(r"^task: (.*)$", ex["prog_text"], re.M).group(1)
   209	    inp = re.search(r"^input: (.*)$", ex["prog_text"], re.M).group(1)
   210	    ans = re.search(r"^answer: (.*)$", ex["prog_text"], re.M).group(1)
   211	    return {"task": task, "input": inp, "answer_line": ans, "events": events, "registers": regs, "tape": tape,
   212	            "anchors": anc, "final": {r["name"]: r["name_of"][final[r["name"]]] for r in regs},
   213	            "trace": [{r["name"]: r["name_of"][st[r["name"]]] for r in regs} for _, _, st in steps],
   214	            "answer": ex["answer"]}, None
   215	
   216	
   217	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ token sequences
   218	
   219	def _enc(tok, text):
   220	    return list(tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False).ids)
   221	
   222	
   223	def program_ids(tok, lit):
   224	    nl = _enc(tok, "\n")
   225	    ids = _enc(tok, f"<program>\ntask: {lit['task']}\ninput: {lit['input']}\n")
   226	    before, after = lit["anchors"]
   227	    ids += _enc(tok, "tape: after " + json.dumps(before, ensure_ascii=False) + " until "
   228	                + json.dumps(after, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
   229	    for r in lit["registers"]:
   230	        ids += _enc(tok, f"register {r['name']}: start") + [name_id(tok, r["start"])] + nl
   231	        for s, e, t in r["rows"]:
   232	            ids += [name_id(tok, s), name_id(tok, e), name_id(tok, t)] + nl
   233	    ids += _enc(tok, f"answer: {lit['answer_line']}\n</program>\n<exec/>")
   234	    return ids
   235	
   236	
   237	def final_ids(tok, lit, final=None):
   238	    final = final or lit["final"]
   239	    ids = _enc(tok, "\n<final>")
   240	    for r in lit["registers"]:
   241	        ids += _enc(tok, f" {r['name']}=") + [name_id(tok, final[r["name"]])]
   242	    return ids + _enc(tok, " </final>\n")
   243	
   244	
   245	def run_ids(tok, lit):
   246	## P2-v2 registration
   247	
   248	Registered before any P2-v2 spend. P1 and P3 are not rerun. The job is `p2v2-savant-r3` in `pr_v2.py`: Savant r3 only, seed 20261028, adapter `/arvol/arith-s1r2/adapter_step500`. One A10G job, `max_seconds` 28723. `pr_modal.reservation` of that limit is $14.999818, the largest integer limit at or under $15. The ledger has $1.8692 settled, so the reservation also fits the $25 cap.
   249	
   250	The trained format is a `pb_families.cycle` program (train split, one register, at most 16 rows) laid out by `br_core.layout` and `pb_lit`. Those builders are imported. The tape is the only part constructed here, so that one held row is first used at a chosen event index. The bare format is the 16-row machine in `pr_data.py`: `encode_rows`, then a stream of `(state, event)`.
   251	
   252	Draws are seeds k = 0, 1, … until a format has four passing draws or k reaches 16. The delay-16 gate is a pin of every row, with margin above 0, and a teacher-forced run of 16 events that is correct at every step. Trained Π is the program, without the chat prompt, positions restarted. Bare Π is the encoded table. The bare graph has no covering walk of length 16, so its 16-event run is `events_for_delay(16)` and the pin is what reads every row. A format with no passing draw is out of distribution and is dropped from the long-delay analysis.
   253	
   254	τ is 16, 256, 2048, 8192, and τ_max. τ_max is the largest even event index, at least 16, that still fits `max_position_embeddings` read from the config, with four later reuses of the held row. It is computed per draw. For the bare table the fit is `pi_len + 2 * (τ + 1 + 8) ≤ max_position_embeddings`; on a 40960 window and a 64-token table that is 20438. For the trained format the prompt contains the tape, so the fit is `br_core.layout`'s token length. A fixed τ larger than that draw's τ_max is dropped, and τ_max is added when it is not already in the list.
   255	
   256	Each τ has two arms. Teacher forcing writes the true states, so the held row is exposed. Free running writes the model's prediction. An error at an event index strictly before τ is "diverged before exposure" and that arm has no first-use margin. The step log is `(step, event-token position, state written, event, prediction, true destination, margin)`, plus the pin bit on errors. The margin is positive when the true destination is the argmax.
   257	
   258	At every full-history error the same `(state, event)` is read with `pb_pin.prefill` and `pb_pin.tick`. A pair is forwarded once; the pin does not depend on the run. The trained pin's Π is the program. The bare pin's Π is the table.
   259	
   260	The corruption test replaces the swap test. On a teacher-forced trajectory one earlier destination of the held row is overwritten with a different state token. The next use is g ≥ 2 events later, so the overwritten token is not the state token of that reuse. The token distance is `g * stride − 1`. Both formats have stride 2, so the aims 64, 512 and 4096 are the distances 65, 513 and 4097. The largest g that fits is included when its distance is not already one of those. The uncorrupted twin is scored at the same reuse. `follow_h` is the overwritten token, `follow_t` is the table's destination, and neither is the rest.
   261	
   262	The fall is the median, across the passing draws, of the teacher-forced first-use margin at each exposed τ. It falls when every longer τ has a strictly smaller median. The flat-or-rising falsifier is that comparison in the trained format. Fewer than two τ is untested. Free-run margins are reported and are not that falsifier.
   263	
   264	At the shortest recorded corruption distance, the copy rate is the fraction of draws with `follow_h` 1 and the control error rate is the fraction whose uncorrupted twin is wrong. The copy claim holds when the copy rate is higher. The falsifier fires when `follow_h` is 0 at every recorded distance. The pin claim holds when the pin is correct on every recorded full-history error. Its falsifier fires when the pin is wrong on every one of them. No errors leaves the pin claim untested.
   265	
   266	Work order is the gate, then for each passing draw the teacher-forced ladder, the corruption distances, and the free-runs, trained format before bare. The process stops 90 seconds before `max_seconds` and keeps the cells already written. Step logs are separate json files. The summary is `runs.json`. These results stay unaudited.
   267	
   268	## P2-v2 results
   269	
   270	Unaudited. An independent audit follows before anyone believes them.
   271

  1160	        lines.append("Corrupted history was copied at least once.")
  1161	    lines.append("")
  1162	    pin_n = sum(int(c.get("pin_n") or 0) for c in cells.values())
  1163	    pin_err = sum(int(c.get("pin_err") or 0) for c in cells.values())
  1164	    lines.append(f"Pinned twin on full-history errors: wrong {pin_err} of {pin_n}.")
  1165	    if pin_n == 0:

  1167	    elif pin_err == 0:
  1168	        lines.append("The pinned twin is correct on every recorded full-history error.")
  1169	    elif pin_err == pin_n:
  1170	        lines.append("The pinned twin errs on every recorded full-history error. Falsifier fires.")
  1171	    else:
  1172	        lines.append("The pinned twin is correct on some recorded errors and wrong on others.")
  1173	    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"

   245	def run_ids(tok, lit):
   246	    ids = []

   253	def example_ids(tok, ex, lit, mode, im_end):
   254	    """(ids, labels) for prompt + response. Harness-written spans (`<final>…</final>`, `<run>` opener) are inputs,
   255	    not targets."""
   256	    prompt = _enc(tok, ex["prompt"])
   257	    prog = program_ids(tok, lit)
   258	    ids, labels = prompt + prog, [-100] * len(prompt) + prog
   259	    if mode == "pinned":
   260	        fin = final_ids(tok, lit)
   261	        ids += fin
   262	        labels += [-100] * len(fin)
   263	    else:
   264	        opener = _enc(tok, "\n<run>\n")
   265	        run = run_ids(tok, lit) + _enc(tok, "\n</run>\n")
   266	        ids += opener + run
   267	        labels += [-100] * len(opener) + run
   268	    ans = _enc(tok, lit["answer"]) + [im_end]
   269	    return ids + ans, labels + ans

   434	def cycle(rng, L, split="any", phrasing=None, fmt=None):
   435	    thing, noun, vals = rng.choice(CYCLES)
   436	    events = rng.choice([["next", "back", "stay"], ["tick", "hold"], ["forward", "backward"]])
   437	    start = rng.choice(vals)
   438	    order = ", ".join(vals)
   439	    legend = {"next": "'next' moves one step forward, 'back' one step backward, 'stay' does nothing",
   440	              "tick": "'tick' moves one step forward, 'hold' does nothing",
   441	              "forward": "'forward' moves one step forward, 'backward' one step backward"}[events[0]]
   442	    phr = [
   443	        ("A {t} cycles through {o} and then starts over. It is on {s0}. Commands ({lg}): {s}. What {n} is it on now?", "It is on {c}."),
   444	        ("Cycle: {o} (after the last comes the first again). Start: {s0}. Steps ({lg}):\n{s}\nWhich {n} do we end on?", "We end on {c}."),
   445	        ("The {t} shows {s0}. Its order is {o}, repeating. Then this happens ({lg}): {s}. What does it show?", "It shows {c}."),
   446	        ("Starting at {s0} in the repeating order {o}, follow these commands ({lg}): {s}. Final {n}?", "The final {n} is {c}."),
   447	        ("A {t} goes {o}, round and round. Now at {s0}. Log ({lg}): {s}. Where is it?", "It is at {c}."),
   448	        ("{t}: {o} on a loop; current {n} {s0}. Input ({lg}): {s}. Result?", "The result is {c}."),
   449	        ("I keep pressing buttons on a {t} that loops through {o}. It started on {s0}. My presses ({lg}): {s}. What {n} is selected?", "{c} is selected."),
   450	        ("Rotate through {o} (wrapping around) starting from {s0}: {s} ({lg}). Which {n}?", "The {n} is {c}."),
   451	    ]
   452	    i = phrasing if phrasing is not None else _pick(rng, len(phr), split)
   453	    fmts = ["comma", "space", "lines"]
   454	    f = fmt or _pick_fmt(rng, fmts, split)
   455	    items = [rng.choice(events) for _ in range(L)]
   456	    q, ans = phr[i]
   457	    ans = ans.replace("{n}", noun)
   458	    rules = [(e, "c+1 wrap") for e in events if e in ("next", "tick", "forward")] + \
   459	            [(e, "c-1 wrap") for e in events if e in ("back", "backward")]
   460	    prog = format_program(f"position of the {thing} in its cycle", "the commands in order", events, "",
   461	                          [("c", " ".join(vals))], [("c", start)], rules, ans)
   462	    text = q.format(t=thing, o=order, s0=start, lg=legend, s=render_stream(items, f), n=noun)
   463	    return _finish("cycle", i, f, text, prog, items, len(phr), fmts, {"cycle": thing})
   464	
   465	
   466	def _passage(rng, L, target):
   467	    rate = rng.uniform(0.03, 0.15)
   468	    words, out = [], []
   469	    for j in range(L):
   470	        w = target if rng.random() < rate else rng.choice(COMMON_WORDS)
   471	        words.append(w)
   472	    sentence_start = True
   473	    for j, w in enumerate(words):
   474	        shown = w.capitalize() if sentence_start else w
   475	        sentence_start = False
   476	        r = rng.random()
   477	        if j == L - 1 or r < 0.07:
   478	            shown += "."
   479	            sentence_start = True
   480	        elif r < 0.12:
   481	            shown += ","
   482	        out.append(shown)
   483	    items = [s.rstrip(".,") for s in out]
   484	    return " ".join(out), items

   487	def wordcount(rng, L, split="any", phrasing=None, fmt=None):
   488	    target = rng.choice(["the", "and", "a", "of", "to", "in", "it", "cat", "red", "time"])
   489	    width = 2 if L < 100 else 3 if L < 1000 else 4
   490	    hi = 10 ** width - 1
     1	"""
     2	PROMPTBOX training and evaluation examples.
     3	
     4	  interp   a random program (random register and state names, random alphabets, random rules) in the user turn with
     5	           an input; the answer is check table, `certified`, run, answer.
     6	  family   an ordinary question from pb_families; the answer is program, check table, `certified`, run, answer.
     7	  verify   either of the above with 1-3 check rows corrupted in the context (the wrong value is never a training
     8	           target); the target is ✗ on each corrupted row, then `not certified`, a fresh correct check table, and the
     9	           rest as usual.
    10	  answer-only twin of any example: same prompt, the plain-words answer as the whole response.
    11	
    12	Responses are tokenized as whole strings; masked character spans become label -100.
    13	"""
    14	
    15	from __future__ import annotations
    16	
    17	import math
    18	import random
    19	
    20	from pb_families import COMMON_WORDS, FAMILY_MAX_L, make
    21	from pb_lang import (CHECK_BAD, NUMBER_WORDS, ProgramError, check_row_prefix, check_row_text, check_rows,
    22	                     format_program, parse_program, render_answer, run_program, run_text)
    23	
    24	SYSTEMLESS_TEMPLATE = "<|im_start|>user\n{q}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n\n</think>\n\n"
    25	
    26	
    27	def chat_prompt(question):
    28	    return SYSTEMLESS_TEMPLATE.format(q=question)
    29	
    30	
    31	def general_prompt_pool():
    32	    """Instruction prompts (Alpaca and Dolly, no extra input) split once: training self-distillation vs evaluation."""
    33	    from datasets import load_dataset
    34	    rows = [r["instruction"].strip() for r in load_dataset("tatsu-lab/alpaca", split="train") if not r["input"]]
    35	    rows += [r["instruction"].strip() for r in load_dataset("databricks/databricks-dolly-15k", split="train")
    36	             if not r["context"]]
    37	    rows = sorted({r for r in rows if 10 <= len(r) <= 1500})
    38	    random.Random(99).shuffle(rows)
    39	    return {"train": rows[:-2000], "eval": rows[-2000:]}

    42	class Pools:
    43	    """Single-token building blocks, checked against the tokenizer."""
    44	
    45	    def __init__(self, tok):
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	# Roles. Names are filled from the tokenizer's held-out pool; the transition structure is not.
    11	STATES = ("s0", "s1", "s2", "q")
    12	EVENTS = ("loop", "enter", "back", "star")
    13	HELD = ("q", "star")
    14	# (q, star) goes to s2. The swap test changes that one destination to s0.
    15	HELD_DEST = "s2"
    16	SWAP_DEST = "s0"
    17	# Four-row table for the swap grid, held row first, so the value token sits near the start.
    18	SWAP_KEYS = (("q", "star"), ("s0", "loop"), ("s1", "loop"), ("s0", "enter"))
    19	# Events before the delayed first use. Length 9, ends in s0. See events_for_delay.
    20	PREAMBLE = ("loop", "loop", "loop", "loop", "enter", "back", "enter", "back", "back")
    21	REUSES = 4
    22	SEED = 20261028
    23	
    24	DELTA = {
    25	    ("s0", "loop"): "s1",
    26	    ("s1", "loop"): "s0",
    27	    ("s2", "loop"): "s2",
    28	    ("q", "loop"): "q",
    29	    ("s0", "enter"): "q",
    30	    ("s1", "enter"): "q",
    31	    ("s2", "enter"): "q",
    32	    ("q", "enter"): "q",
    33	    ("s0", "back"): "s0",
    34	    ("s1", "back"): "s1",
    35	    ("s2", "back"): "s2",
    36	    ("q", "back"): "s0",
    37	    ("s0", "star"): "s1",
    38	    ("s1", "star"): "s0",
    39	    ("s2", "star"): "s0",
    40	    ("q", "star"): HELD_DEST,
    41	}
    42	
    43	
    44	def rows_state_major():
    45	    """All 16 rows. The held row is placed first so its value token is near position 0."""
    46	    rest = [(s, e, DELTA[(s, e)]) for s in STATES for e in EVENTS if (s, e) != HELD]
    47	    return [(HELD[0], HELD[1], HELD_DEST)] + rest
    48	
    49	
    50	def swap_rows():
    51	    return [(s, e, DELTA[(s, e)]) for s, e in SWAP_KEYS]
    52	
    53	
    54	def encode_rows(rows, id_of, nl):
    55	    """Token ids of ` {s} {e} {t}\\n` per row, and the span of each row. id_of maps a role to one token id."""
    56	    ids, spans = [], []
    57	    for s, e, t in rows:
    58	        spans.append({"s": s, "e": e, "t": t, "pos_s": len(ids), "pos_e": len(ids) + 1, "pos_t": len(ids) + 2})
    59	        ids.extend((id_of[s], id_of[e], id_of[t], nl))
    60	    return ids, spans
    61	
    62	
    63	def span_of(spans, key):
    64	    for sp in spans:
    65	        if (sp["s"], sp["e"]) == key:
    96	def trace(events, start="s0"):
    97	    s = start
    98	    out = []
    99	    for e in events:
   100	        nxt = DELTA[(s, e)]
   101	        out.append((s, e, nxt))
   102	        s = nxt
   103	    return out
   104	
   105	
   106	def events_for_delay(tau, reuses=REUSES):
   107	    """Events whose trace uses (q, star) for the first time at index tau, after the other rows have been reused.
   108	
   109	    tau is even and at least 16. `reuses` further (enter, star) pairs follow, so the held row is used again.
   110	    """
   111	    if tau < 16 or tau % 2:
   112	        raise ValueError(f"tau must be an even integer >= 16, got {tau}")
   113	    n_loop = tau - 10  # tau - 1 - len(PREAMBLE); even when tau is even
   114	    if n_loop < 0 or n_loop % 2:
   115	        raise ValueError(f"loop count {n_loop} for tau {tau}")
   116	    events = list(PREAMBLE) + ["loop"] * n_loop + ["enter", "star"]
   117	    events += ["enter", "star"] * reuses
   118	    if len(events) != tau + 1 + 2 * reuses:
   119	        raise ValueError("event length")
   120	    return events
   121	
   122	
   123	def held_uses(events):
   124	    return [i for i, (s, e, _t) in enumerate(trace(events)) if (s, e) == HELD]
   125	
   126	
    97	        raise RuntimeError("swap changed more than one token")
    98	    return out
    99	
   100	
   101	def names_for(pool, k, seed):
   102	    words = random.Random(seed + k).sample(list(pool["test"]), 8)
   103	    roles = list(pr_data.STATES + pr_data.EVENTS)
   104	    names = dict(zip(roles, words))
   105	    id_of = {role: int(pool["id_of"][word]) for role, word in names.items()}
   106	    if len(set(id_of.values())) != 8:
   107	        raise RuntimeError("name ids are not distinct")
   108	    return names, id_of, int(pool["nl"])
   109	
   110	
   111	def _cell(value):
   874	def _table_from_pool(pool, tok, k, seed):
   875	    names, id_of, nl = names_for(pool, k, seed)
   876	    text, fid = choose_filler(tok, set(id_of.values()) | {nl})
   877	    return {"k": k, "names": names, "id_of": id_of, "nl": nl, "filler": fid, "filler_text": text}

    59	def load_payloads(root):
    60	    root = pathlib.Path(root)
    61	    plan_path = root / "plan.json"
    62	    if not plan_path.is_file():
    63	        raise SystemExit(f"no plan.json in {root}")
    64	    plan = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    65	    payloads = []
    66	    for job in plan["jobs"]:
    67	        path = root / job["id"] / "runs.json"
    68	        if not path.is_file():
    69	            raise SystemExit(f"missing {path}")
    70	        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
    71	        if not payload.get("complete"):
    72	            raise SystemExit(f"{job['id']} is not complete")
    73	        payload["job_id"] = job["id"]
    74	        payloads.append(payload)
    75	    return plan, payloads
    76	
    77

   569	def analyse(plan, payloads):
   570	    models = {p["model"]: analyse_model(p) for p in payloads if p.get("p1")}
   571	    past = _past_horizon(models)
   572	    p3 = [item for item in (_p3_model(p) for p in payloads) if item]
   573	    return {"plan": plan, "models": models, "p2": _p2(payloads, past), "p3": {m["model"]: m for m in p3},
   574	            "past_horizon": past}

     1	"""
     2	Attention hooks for PREDICT.
     3	
     4	RoPE (Qwen3). A copied Qwen3Attention forward from transformers 5.19. When every scale is 1
     5	and the recorder is off, the original forward is called instead. Scaling a query head by τ
     6	multiplies that head's pre-softmax scores by τ, because those scores are q·k with no added
     7	bias. The recorder keeps only the last query's scores, in chunks, so a 32k context does not
     8	materialise a head-by-sequence score matrix for every head at once.
     9	
    10	ALiBi (BLOOM). The stock forward is unchanged. The wrapper reads the last query's attention
    11	probabilities and the ALiBi slopes off the bias tensor the model already built.
    12	
    13	CIRCUITS' destination-reading committee (survey-final t-readers, 31 heads). L7.H7 is the base
    14	model's row finder and is not in that set. L24.H23 is the committee's top head.
    15	"""
    16	
    17	from __future__ import annotations
    18	
    19	# (layer, head), CIRCUITS survey rank order.
    20	COMMITTEE = (
    21	    (24, 23), (22, 23), (22, 22), (20, 21), (24, 22), (23, 4), (29, 11), (24, 5),

     1	"""
     2	PROMPTBOX program language: the reference semantics behind training data, certification and the audit.
     3	
     4	A program is a set of registers with finite ordered domains (words, or fixed-width numbers). On each input event every
     5	register gets a new value computed from the values all registers had before the event. Text form, as the model
     6	writes it:
     7	
     8	    <program>
     9	    task: where the ball ends up after the swaps
    10	    input: the swaps listed after "Swaps:"
    11	    events: LM MR LR
    12	    ball: left middle right
    13	    start: ball=left
    14	    on LM: ball left>middle middle>left
    15	    on MR: ball middle>right right>middle
    16	    on LR: ball left>right right>left
    17	    answer: The ball is under the {ball} cup.
    18	    </program>
    19	
    20	Domains: `name: w1 w2 ...` (words, ordinal by position) or `name: lo..hi [step s] [| extra ...]` (numbers written
    21	zero-padded to the width of hi, plus absorbing extra words). Events: listed tokens, optionally `| other word` or
    22	`| other letter` (a class for anything unlisted), or `digit pairs` (each event is two digits a b).
    23	
    24	Rules: `on EVENT: part, part, ...` (or `on each digit d:` / `on each pair a b:`). A part is `reg OP [if other=V]`:
    25	a table `reg a>b c>d` (unlisted values stay), a shift `reg+k MODE` / `reg-k MODE` with MODE wrap, cap, stay or
    26	`else V`, a set `reg=V`, or an expression `reg=(...) mod m` over register values (numbers, or a word's position),
    27	d, a, b and + - * //. A register's parts are tried in order and the first whose condition holds applies; with none,
    28	the register keeps its value. Conditions read the values from before the event. `each OP` applies OP to every
    29	register (all registers then share one domain).
    30	
    31	Answers: `answer: TEXT` or `answer: if reg=V [and reg=V]: TEXT ... else: TEXT`, with {reg} (the value, numbers
    32	unpadded), {reg#} (its numeric meaning) and {reg<} (the register's values over the run, last first, read as one
    33	number).
    34	
    35	Check table: for every register, every combination of the values its step reads (its own value, the registers in its
    73	def _finish(family, phrasing, fmt, question, program_text, items, n_phr, fmts, meta=None):
    74	    prog = parse_program(program_text)
    75	    steps, final = run_program(prog, items)
    76	    held_p = family in TRAIN_FAMILIES and (phrasing == "bbh" or phrasing >= n_phr - 2)
    77	    held_f = family in TRAIN_FAMILIES and len(fmts) > 1 and fmt == fmts[-1]
    78	    answer = render_answer(prog, final, steps)
    79	    meta = dict(meta or {}, key=answer_key(prog, final, answer))
    80	    return Instance(family, phrasing, fmt, len(items), question, program_text, items, answer, final, held_p, held_f,
    81	                    meta)
    82	
    83	
    84	def answer_key(prog, final, answer):
    85	    """What a correct plain-words answer must say: Yes/No, an option letter, a long number read off the run, or the
    86	    values the answer template prints, in order."""
    87	    case_text = answer_case(prog, final)
    88	    key = {"yesno": True if answer.startswith("Yes") else False if answer.startswith("No") else None,
    89	           "words": [], "numbers": [], "number_domains": [], "word_domains": [], "text": None}
    90	    option = re.search(r"\(([A-Z])\)", answer)
    91	    if option:
    92	        key["option"] = option.group(1)
    93	        return key
    94	    if re.search(r"\{[a-z][a-z0-9_]*<\}", case_text):
    95	        key["digits"] = re.findall(r"\d+", answer)[-1]
    96	        return key
    97	    places = {r.name: re.search(r"\{" + re.escape(r.name) + r"[}#<]", case_text) for r in prog.registers}
    98	    shown = sorted(((m.start(), r) for r in prog.registers if (m := places[r.name])), key=lambda t: t[0])
    99	    key["labels"], key["slots"] = [], []
   100	    for pos, r in shown:
   101	        v = final[r.name]
   102	        before = case_text[:pos].rstrip(" :=(").split()
   103	        label = before[-1].lower().strip(",.") if before else ""
   104	        key["labels"].append(label)
   105	        if "{" + r.name + "#}" in case_text or (r.kind == "num" and v not in r.extras):
   106	            num = r.nums[r.index(v)] if "{" + r.name + "#}" in case_text else int(v)
   107	            domain = [n for n in r.nums if n is not None]
   108	            key["numbers"].append(num)
   109	            key["number_domains"].append(domain)
   110	            key["slots"].append({"label": label, "kind": "num", "value": num, "domain": domain})
   111	        else:
   112	            key["words"].append(v)
   113	            key["word_domains"].append([x for x in r.values])
   114	            key["slots"].append({"label": label, "kind": "word", "value": v, "domain": [x for x in r.values]})
   115	    if key["yesno"] is None and not shown:
   116	        key["text"] = answer
   316	   ],
   317	   "gpu": "A10G",
   318	   "gpu_actual": "NVIDIA A10",
   319	   "job_id": "p2v2-savant-r3",

     1	"""
     2	Modal launcher for PREDICT. Own app `polyformer-predict`, own volume `polyformer-predict-results`.
     3	The UNIVERSAL HF cache is mounted read-only at /hf for compute jobs and read-write for the download
     4	job. ARITH's volume is mounted read-only at /arvol. Ledger cap $25, recorded before any spend.
     5	
     6	GPU jobs request Modal's `A10` and are ledgered as A10G at the A10G list price. Importing never launches.
     7	
     8	    python research/predict/pr_modal.py init
     9	    python research/predict/pr_modal.py plan --name smoke --jobs research/predict/jobs/smoke.json

    41	def atomic_json(path, value):
    42	    path = pathlib.Path(path)
    43	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    44	    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + f".{os.getpid()}.tmp")
    45	    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=1, sort_keys=True, default=str))
    46	    os.replace(tmp, path)

    66	def source_files():
    67	    files = [ROOT / n for n in OWN] + [PB_ROOT / n for n in PB_SHIP]
    68	    missing = [str(p) for p in files if not p.is_file()]
    69	    if missing:
    70	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"source files missing: {missing}")
    71	    return files

    74	def remote_name(p):
    75	    if p.parent == ROOT:
    76	        return "pr/" + p.name
    77	    return "pb/" + p.name

    79	
    80	def sources():
    81	    return {remote_name(p): sha256_bytes(p.read_bytes()) for p in source_files()}

    84	def job_rate(job):
    85	    if job.get("gpu") == "none":
    86	        return float(job["cpu"]) * CPU_RATE + float(job["mem_gib"]) * RAM_RATE
    87	    return GPU_RATE[job["gpu"]] + CPU_MAX * CPU_RATE + RAM_GIB_MAX * RAM_RATE
    88	
    89	
    90	def reservation(job):
    91	    if job.get("gpu") == "none":
    92	        return round(job_rate(job) * (job["max_seconds"] + DOWNLOAD_STARTUP), 6)
    93	    return round(job_rate(job) * (job["max_seconds"] + WRAPPER_SECONDS + STARTUP_SECONDS), 6)

   120	def _norm(text):
   121	    return " ".join("".join(c.lower() if c.isalnum() else " " for c in text).split())

   124	def last_number(text):
   125	    """The last number written in a text, digit groups joined across commas, spaces and thin spaces."""
   126	    runs = re.findall(r"\d(?:[\d,\u2009\u202f ]*\d)?", text)
   127	    return re.sub(r"\D", "", runs[-1]) if runs else None
   128

   130	def last_option(text):
   131	    found = re.findall(r"\(([A-Z])\)", text)
   132	    return found[-1] if found else None

   134	
   135	def answer_correct(key, text):
   136	    """Whether a plain-words answer states exactly what the key requires (and nothing contradicting it)."""
   137	    t = text.strip()
   138	    if key.get("option") is not None:
   139	        return last_option(t) == key["option"]
   140	    if key.get("digits") is not None:
   141	        return last_number(t) == key["digits"]
   142	    if key.get("text") is not None:
   143	        return _norm(t) == _norm(key["text"])
   144	    if key["yesno"] is not None:
   145	        low = t.lower().lstrip("*\"' ")
   146	        if not low.startswith("yes" if key["yesno"] else "no"):
   147	            return False
   148	    slots = key.get("slots") or []
   149	    if len(slots) == 1:
   150	        return _one_slot(slots[0], t)
   151	    if slots:
   152	        return _many_slots(slots, t)
   153	    return True

   157	    """Single letters keep their case (so the article "a" is not the value "A"); words compare in lower case."""
   158	    return word if len(word) == 1 else word.lower()

   227	def _pick(rng, n, split, n_held=2):
   228	    """Phrasing index: training splits avoid the last n_held."""
   229	    if split in ("train", "heldout_format"):
   230	        return rng.randrange(n - n_held)
   231	    if split in ("heldout", "heldout_phrasing"):
   232	        return rng.randrange(n - n_held, n)
   233	    return rng.randrange(n)
   234

   236	def _pick_fmt(rng, fmts, split):
   237	    if split in ("train", "heldout_phrasing") and len(fmts) > 1:
   238	        return rng.choice(fmts[:-1])
   239	    if split in ("heldout", "heldout_format") and len(fmts) > 1:
   240	        return fmts[-1]
   241	    return rng.choice(fmts)

The recorded arithmetic mostly matches. The strongest problems are **unequal gates, self-loop probes that do not establish table use, and pin success selected by the gate itself**. No files changed.

I used an independent Python script: load `runs.json` and the step files; count `prediction != true`; identify first occurrences of each held `(state,event)`; group recorded margins by arm and τ; take medians; sum corruption outcomes and pin bits. This recomputes statistics from records, not logits from model weights. Raw summaries are minified on [runs.json:1](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/results/p2v2-savant-r3/runs.json:1).

**Checklist findings**

1. **OK — imported harness format.** The executed builder calls `pb_lit.literal(ex, tok)` and `br_core.layout(tok, ex, lit)` at [pr_v2.py:330](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:330) and [pr_v2.py:335](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:335). The shared layout checks `"program layout does not match pb_lit.program_ids"` and uses `pb_lit.run_ids` at [br_core.py:104](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/br_core.py:104). This establishes the plain sequence format. It does **not** establish that the input rendering was trained: `fmt="lines"` is forced at [pr_v2.py:311](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:311), while the family marks its last format held out at [pb_families.py:77](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/promptbox/pb_families.py:77); cycle’s last format is `"lines"` at [pb_families.py:453](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/promptbox/pb_families.py:453).

2. **OK — executed first use and scoring alignment.** Queries use `epos = rs + k * stride` and target `ids[epos + 1]` at [pr_v2.py:359](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:359) and [pr_v2.py:366](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:366). Free execution computes `true = dest_of.get((state_id, event))` and checks the actual held pair at [pr_v2.py:608](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:608). All recorded exposures occur at τ; the divergent run correctly has none. Margins are target minus best other vocabulary logit: `"margin = top - float(torch.max(masked))"` / `"float(lg[target]) - top"` at [pr_job.py:391](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_job.py:391) and [pr_job.py:394](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_job.py:394).

3. **OK mechanically; DESIGN FLAW mechanistically — corruption.** The constructor requires `uses != [first, first + g]` to reject a tape at [pr_v2.py:169](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:169). Replacement candidates exclude `true_out`, and exactly one assignment changes the input: `dirty[out_pos] = wrong` at [pr_v2.py:994](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:994) and [pr_v2.py:999](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:999). I reconstructed all 16 event trajectories and verified the two visits and recorded distances.

   However, held-row selection prioritizes self-loops: `transit(se[0], se[1]) != se[0]` sorts first when false at [pr_v2.py:239](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:239). All four selected rows are hold/stay self-loops; the prompt explicitly says these commands “do nothing” at [pb_families.py:439](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/promptbox/pb_families.py:439). Thus the correct answer is already the current state. Moreover, `follow_t = int(pd == reuse["true"])` measures agreement, **not evidence of table consultation** ([pr_v2.py:1014](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:1014)).

4. **OK mechanically; DESIGN FLAW as independent evidence — pinned twin.** Errors call `pin.query(state_id, event, true)` at [pr_v2.py:620](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:620); shared `tick` feeds exactly `[[s_id, e_id]]` at [pb_pin.py:245](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/promptbox/pb_pin.py:245). But admission already requires `pin_wrong == 0` over every row ([pr_v2.py:781](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:781), [pr_v2.py:801](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:801)). The three corrections survive; independent evidence that pinning generally rescues failures does not.

5. **OK for causal target exclusion; DESIGN FLAW for broader leakage/generalization claims.** The supplied program contains every destination, and the prompt contains the whole event tape; this is an oracle-program execution test. `"ids = prompt + prog + opener + run + closer"` appears at [br_core.py:108](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/br_core.py:108). TF supplies prior correct states; free runs supply predictions ([pr_v2.py:633](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/predict/pr_v2.py:633)). No final-answer string is appended by this layout; although `literal` retains `"answer": ex["answer"]`, the program emits `answer_line`, the template ([pb_lit.py:214](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/promptbox/pb_lit.py:214), [pb_lit.py:233](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/predict/research/promptbox/pb_lit.py:233)).
